# (enum job|internship|stipend_internship, matched against the row's flattened value). Renamed the key

# (distinct from the UUID-based Applications/Student-list endpoints). FE-only, one line, no migration.

# Enhancement: Employment — multiple entries, removed Duration, close-with-mandatory-completion-proof.
# The Employment tab changed from a single record to a LIST: students add multiple entries (type, company,
# designation, package — Duration REMOVED). Adding an entry now REQUIRES a mandatory offer-letter document
# (POST /me/employments is multipart; controller 400 FILE_REQUIRED if missing; stored in the existing
# offer_letter_url column — no extra migration). Each entry can be Closed, which REQUIRES uploading a completion
# proof document; once closed it is locked (read-only). Both active & closed entries are deletable (confirm).
# Entry cards show the Offer letter (and, when closed, the Completion proof) as links.
# Model relaxed from one-to-one (student_id @unique) to one-to-many. The Employment tab + admin student detail
# were updated to render the list. is_currently_working is kept (= status==='active') for admin display.
#
# Adversity handled: (1) API shape employment(object)→employments(array) — updated every reader (getMyProfile,
# admin getStudentDetail + include, FE ApiStudentProfile + types/admin, admin dialogs); (2) recalcProfileCompletion
# does NOT read employment (verified) so completion scoring unaffected; (3) mandatory completion proof enforced
# both FE (close dialog disables confirm until a file is chosen) and BE (400 FILE_REQUIRED); (4) closed entries
# locked + a second close rejected (422 EMPLOYMENT_ALREADY_CLOSED); (5) existing single rows survive as the first
# entry; legacy offer_letter_url/business_proof_url columns left untouched.
#
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED — run `npx prisma migrate dev`. schema.prisma (CurrentEmployment):
#   - DROP the `student_id` @unique (now one-to-many) + add @@index([student_id])
#   - DROP column `duration`
#   - ADD columns: status (VarChar default 'active'), closed_at (DateTime?), completion_proof_url (Text),
#     completion_proof_name (VarChar), created_at (default now())
#   - Student relation `current_employment CurrentEmployment?` → `employments CurrentEmployment[]`
#   Then restart the backend (tsx watch) — the routes changed to /me/employments(/:id/close|delete).
docs/silveroak_backend/prisma/schema.prisma

# Enhancement: Student placement opt-out — global + per-posting-type, with reason, confirm-lock, history.
# Students can declare "not interested in placement" entirely (global switch) or per posting type, from a
# new Profile → "Placement" tab. Opting out requires a REASON + confirm; re-enabling is self-service
# (simple confirm). Every enable/disable is recorded in a history table (shown collapsibly in the tab).
# "Show but do not allow register": postings of an opted-out type stay VISIBLE but apply/register-interest
# is blocked — FE disables the Apply button with a reason; BE guards apply() + registerInterests().
#
# Adversity handled: (1) global overrides per-type (enforcement checks global first; per-type switches
# greyed while global is ON); (2) reason required only on opt-out (Zod superRefine + FE dialog gate);
# (3) no posting visibility change — only the apply/interest guards + Apply button; (4) coexists with the
# positive InterestRegistration concept (separate table) — registerInterests also blocks opted-out types;
# (5) existing applications kept (opt-out blocks only NEW applies); (6) history stores a posting_type_label
# snapshot + plain id (no FK) so it survives a posting-type rename/delete; per-type pref FK onDelete:Cascade.
#
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED — run `npx prisma migrate dev` (additive). schema.prisma changes:
#   - Student: + placement_opt_out (Boolean default false), placement_opt_out_reason (Text), placement_opt_out_at (DateTime?)
#   - new table student_posting_type_preferences (student_id, posting_type_master_id, interested, reason; unique[student_id,type])
#   - new table student_placement_preference_history (scope, posting_type_master_id?, posting_type_label?, interested, reason)
#   - MasterOption back-relation (PostingTypePreference)
#   Then restart the backend (tsx watch) if the new /placement-preferences routes 404.

# Enhancement: Policy ↔ Posting Type linkage + mandatory GLOBAL-policy acceptance gate.
# Policy creation/edit now has an OPTIONAL posting-type selector. A policy with NO posting type =
# "global": global policies (a) pop up at student registration as a hard gate ("only when accepted is
# he registered" — enforced via ProtectedRoute redirect to a new /student/policy-gate until accepted)
# and (b) are listed with accepted/pending status in a new "Policies" tab in the student My Profile.
# Posting-type-LINKED policies are captured for future use and are NOT surfaced to students yet.
# Acceptance simplified to a single "I have read and accept this policy" checkbox.
#
# Adversity handled: (1) placement-apply gate (student.policy_accepted via areAllVisiblePoliciesAccepted)
# scoped to GLOBAL policies only (buildVisiblePolicyWhere + policy buildPolicyWhere) so linked policies
# can't deadlock applying; (2) acceptance schema relaxed (4 sharing consents optional) — safe, nothing
# reads those columns for enforcement; legacy /policy page (posts all-6-true) still valid; (3) FK
# onDelete:SetNull (deleting a posting type just makes its policies global). pending_policy_count added
# to the student profile response to drive the gate without redirect loops on tenants with no global policy.
#
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED — run `npx prisma migrate dev` (additive, nullable). schema.prisma changes:
#   - Policy.posting_type_master_id  String?  + FK posting_type_master -> MasterOption (onDelete: SetNull)
#   - MasterOption.policies_using_as_type  Policy[]  (back-relation "PolicyPostingType")
#   - new @@index([posting_type_master_id]) on policies
#   Then restart the backend (tsx watch) if the new ?global filter / route 404s.

# Enhancement: NOC requests — cleaner company capture + verifiable Company link + optional end date.
# (1) Create form: Company Name is now a dropdown of existing tenant companies with an "Other → type a
#     new one" escape; State is a built-in Indian-states dropdown (+ "Other"); City and Designation are
#     free-text with datalist autocomplete of previously-used values; added optional Company PAN + GST
#     (soft format validation) and one optional Supporting Document upload (PAN card / GST cert / reg
#     proof). (2) NOC now find-or-creates a real Company row (source='student', verification_status=
#     'pending'); when the NOC is issued the linked Company flips to 'verified' (best-effort, can't break
#     issuance). Matched companies are linked, never overwritten (fill-only-if-empty pan/gst/city/state).
#     (3) End Date is no longer mandatory anywhere — FE posts null when blank; BE Zod + Prisma made
#     optional; certificate renderer + all list/detail/admin date renders guard null ("Ongoing").
#     Posting type "where applied" was already shown in both list views (no change). Admin/student NOC
#     detail now surfaces PAN/GST, the supporting-doc link, and an "Added by Student" + company-verified
#     badge. New student-safe read endpoint GET /noc/field-suggestions powers the Name/City/Designation
#     options; new POST /noc/supporting-document upload (mirrors offer-letter). FE compiles clean; BE
#     compiles clean.
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED — schema.prisma:
#   • New enum CompanySource { admin student recruiter import }.
#   • Company: + source (default admin), + verification_status (default verified), + pan, gst, city,
#     state; + noc_requests back-relation. (Defaults chosen so existing companies need NO backfill.)
#   • NocRequest: + company_id (FK Company, onDelete SetNull) + index, + company_pan, company_gst,
#     supporting_document_url, supporting_document_name; end_date changed to NULLABLE (DateTime?).
#   Run: cd docs/silveroak_backend && npx prisma migrate dev  (then restart the backend if new
#   /noc/field-suggestions or /noc/supporting-document routes 404).

# Bugfix: TPO NOC Management table was too wide → the whole page scrolled horizontally, and the
# Posting Type / Status badges were wrapping to multiple lines.
# (1) Kept "Posting Type" and "Status" as separate columns but added `whitespace-nowrap` to those (and
#     the Stage) headers/badges so the text no longer wraps and each column takes the width it needs.
# (2) Root cause of the PAGE scrolling (instead of just the table): SidebarInset is a flex child with
#     no min-width:0, so a wide table forced the whole content area wider than the viewport. Added
#     `min-w-0` to SidebarInset so the table's own overflow-x-auto wrapper scrolls horizontally and the
#     page no longer scrolls left. (min-w-0 only lets the flex child shrink — pages that already fit are
#     unaffected.) FE-only, no migration.
src/pages/admin/AdminNOCManagement.tsx

# Enhancement: Block apply / register-interest when the student has a self-placed NOC for the same posting type.
# A non-rejected self-sourced NOC (pending_faculty/pending_tpo/pending_company_verification/approved/issued)
# now blocks the student from applying to — and registering interest in — postings of that SAME posting type
# (other types stay open; university-drive NOCs never block — those are governed by the offer block).
# BE: new shared guard self-placed-noc-block.ts (assertNoSelfPlacedNoc + getSelfPlacedNocProgramValues),
# called in application.service.apply() (after assertPlacementInterest) and student.service.registerInterests()
# (after the per-type opt-out check). Throws BusinessRuleError 'SELF_PLACED_NOC_BLOCK' (HTTP 422), mirroring
# offer-block.ts / placement-interest.ts. Matches NOC.program (= posting-type MasterOption.value) to the
# posting's type by trimmed/lowercased value. FE: OpportunityDetail surfaces it as a readiness reason
# (disables Apply + explains); the apply/interest 422 message also flows through to the toast as a fallback.
# No migration (query-only).
docs/silveroak_backend/src/shared/utils/self-placed-noc-block.ts

# Enhancement: Student sidebar — "new"/unread dot on Announcements, Circulars, and My Events & Drives.
# The student left-sidebar nav items now show a small primary-colored dot when the surface has new content.
# Announcements uses the real backend unread flag (my_receipt.is_read). Circulars and Drives have no read
# flag, so they use a client-side "new since last visit" heuristic: a per-surface last-seen timestamp in
# localStorage (key sou:lastSeen:<surface>); an item is new when created_at > lastSeen. Opening the
# Circulars/Drives page marks the surface seen (clears the dot). New shared store + combined hook in
# use-student-new-indicators.ts (useSyncExternalStore + module listener set for same-tab reactivity).
# The three list hooks gained an optional `enabled` param (default true) so the sidebar gates the queries to
# students only. Frontend-only — no backend/schema/migration.
src/hooks/use-student-new-indicators.ts

# Enhancement: Event Type is now a tenant-configurable Master (manageable + drives event creation).
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED: schema.prisma — add `event_type` to enum MasterCategory; change Event.type from
#   enum `EventType` to `String @db.VarChar(150)`; DROP the `EventType` enum. Prisma's auto-migration can't
#   cast enum→varchar when the table has data, so two HAND-AUTHORED migrations were added (explicit USING cast
#   preserves existing values — no data loss):
#     prisma/migrations/20260605170000_event_type_master/migration.sql        (ADD VALUE 'event_type')
#     prisma/migrations/20260605170100_event_type_string_column/migration.sql (events.type -> VARCHAR(150) + DROP TYPE)
#   Apply with `npx prisma migrate deploy` (does NOT reset), then restart BE. `migrate dev` also works.
# Backend: masters.service.ts seeds the 5 existing event types as event_type masters (ensureTenantEventTypeSeeded,
#   mirrors noc_type seeding). event.schema.ts loosens create/query `type` from z.enum to z.string().min(1).max(150).
#   event.service.ts drops the EventType cast/import and adds assertValidEventType() — event create/update now
#   reject a `type` that isn't an active event_type master (replaces the DB-enum guarantee).

#   targeting). New useEventTypeOptions hook (mirrors usePostingTypeOptions). New getEventTypeLabel(value) in

# Bugfix: Branch-scoped posting types vanished for students — even students of that branch.
# Root cause: the student Dashboard surfaces postings via "register interest → matching postings"
# (InterestPostingsCard shows a posting only if its type matches a registered interest), and the
# registerable types come from GET /masters?category=posting_type, which getMasters filters by
# matchesStudentTargeting (AND across institute/course/branch/semester). A student's "branch"
# (Student.department) is course-derived, while the scope picker offers granular CRM branch names —
# so a branch selection never matches a student and, via the AND, cancels the otherwise-correct
# institute/course match, hiding the type (and its postings). Institute/course/semester scope work.
# Fix (user choice "branch falls back to course"): added matchesStudentTargetingForMaster in
# student-targeting.ts — same as matchesStudentTargeting but the branch dimension falls back to the
# branch's parent course (target_courses if course-scoped, else student.course vs the branch label);
# getMasters now uses it. Scoped to master visibility ONLY — postings/applications/analytics keep the
# strict matchesStudentTargeting. Backend-only, NO migration, NO frontend change.

# Bugfix: Faculty coordinator scoping — Student Directory empty, Institute/Semester dropdowns not loading, NOC not visible.
# Root cause: faculty visibility matched students by exact, case-sensitive `student.department === faculty.department`.
# faculty.department (free-typed or branches[0]) almost never equals the course-derived Student.department, so the
# scoped student set was empty → Directory showed nothing, Institute/Semester dropdown options (derived from that
# empty set) didn't load (Branch still loaded from the master), and the faculty NOC list was empty. The Add User
# institute/course/branch assignment arrays were stored but ignored.
# Fix: new shared/utils/faculty-scope.ts — resolveFacultyScope(user) (normalized union of department+courses+
# branches + institutes) and studentMatchesFacultyScope(student, scope) (tolerant: student department OR course
# matches a target, branch-falls-back-to-course, institute-gated when assigned), reusing normalizeComparable.
# faculty.service.ts now filters students in-memory with this matcher (getStudents/getStudentById/getDashboard) and
# derives Institute + Semester dropdown options from the SCOPED student set (Branch still from master). noc.service.ts
# filters the faculty NOC list/detail in-memory and scope-checks facultyApprove/rejectNoc with the matcher; createNoc
# now notifies faculty whose assignment covers the student. offer.service.ts faculty filter now honours the
# institute/course/branch arrays (exact-match OR, keeps pagination). Backward-compatible (department-only faculty
# still work, now tolerantly). NO migration. NOTE: if Semester still doesn't populate after this, AcademicProfile.semester
# data is unpopulated (real data may live in Student.current_semester) — a separate data fix.
docs/silveroak_backend/src/shared/utils/faculty-scope.ts

# Bugfix: TPO Admin verification tab (/admin/students?tab=verification) — Posting Type now shows & filters correctly.
# Problem: the "Posting types" line and the top Posting-Type filter were driven by the student's job applications.

# interests. Result: posting type showed "—" (couldn't tell which program the student enrolled in / showed
# interest in) and the filter returned nobody for pending students.

# Bugfix: Super Admin → Security & Access Control → Users — "All Users" search box lost focus after one character.
# Root cause: UserManagementTab has an early `if (usersQuery.isLoading && !usersQuery.data) return <PageLoader/>`,
# and useUsers (use-admin-api.ts) had no placeholderData. Typing a char changed the react-query key → new key had
# no cached data → data undefined + isLoading true → the early return swapped the WHOLE tab (search box included)
# for the loader, unmounting the <input> and stealing focus every keystroke (also flashed on filter/page changes).
# Fix (FE-only, no migration): useUsers now sets `placeholderData: keepPreviousData` (TanStack Query v5) so prior
# results stay while a new key fetches → data never goes undefined → the early loader only fires on the very first
# cold load → the search box stays mounted and keeps focus. The inline "Refreshing users..." indicator
# (isFetching && !isLoading) now covers refetch feedback. No-op for the stable-key callers (stat tiles, audit filter).
src/hooks/use-admin-api.ts

# The verification tab hard-coded verification_status:'pending', and the Pending/Verified/Rejected cards were
# counts only, so rejected students were never listed and had no Verify action. The backend verifyStudent has no
# state-machine guard (it sets whatever status is passed) and verifyStudentMutation already supports verified/rejected
# — so this was a pure UI gap. FE-only (VerificationTab.tsx): the 3 count cards are now clickable status filters
# (highlight active); the list uses the selected status. Per-row + Review-dialog actions are gated by status —
# Verify shows unless already verified; Reject shows unless already rejected (pending: both; rejected: Verify to
# re-verify; verified: Reject). Bulk Verify Selected/All + selection checkboxes show only for pending/rejected
# (not verified). Copy generalized (title "<Status> Students", search/empty text). No backend change, no migration.

# Enhancement: Posting-Type policy acceptance enforced before Show Interest / Apply.
# TPO admins can link a policy to a posting type in the Policy Repository (Policy.posting_type_master_id +
# rich content), but linked policies were surfaced to no student. Now, when a student clicks Show Interest or
# Apply for a posting type, a modal shows the policy(ies) linked to that type with an "I Agree" per policy;
# the action proceeds only after ALL linked policies are accepted. Also enforced server-side. NO MIGRATION
# (PolicyAcceptance already keys on (student_id, policy_id, policy_updated_at) — a linked policy's id fits).
# Backend:
#  - policy.schema.ts: queryPoliciesSchema gains posting_type_master_id.
#  - policy.service.ts: buildPolicyWhere returns a posting type's linked policies when posting_type_master_id
#    is passed (students too), else keeps the global-only clamp; getPolicyById lets a student read an
#    audience-visible LINKED policy's content (was global-only).
#  - student.service.ts: acceptPolicy looks a policy up by id via a new buildAudiencePolicyWhere (audience,
#    no global clamp) so a linked policy can be accepted, and ONLY recomputes the global student.policy_accepted
#    boolean for GLOBAL policies — a linked acceptance never flips it (avoids the documented placement deadlock).
#    registerInterests now asserts the policy for each requested posting type.
#  - shared/utils/posting-type-policy.ts (NEW): assertPostingTypePolicyAccepted(studentId, postingTypeMasterId)
#    — no-op when the type has no linked policy; else throws 422 POSTING_TYPE_POLICY_NOT_ACCEPTED unless every
#    audience-visible linked policy has a current acceptance (mirrors placement-interest.ts). Wired into
#    application.service.apply() (after the per-type guards) + student.service.registerInterests().
# Frontend:
#  - types/policy.ts: PolicyQueryParams gains posting_type_master_id. use-policy-api.ts: usePolicies gains an
#    `enabled` flag. GlobalPolicyCard: new defaultExpanded prop (shows content up-front in the gate).
#  - components/policies/PostingTypePolicyDialog.tsx (NEW): fetches the posting type's linked policies; if none
#    or all already accepted it proceeds transparently (no modal), else shows one GlobalPolicyCard per policy
#    (reuses useAcceptPolicy) and enables Continue only once all are accepted.
#  - OpportunityDetail.tsx: the 3 Apply buttons open the policy gate (onProceed -> the existing ApplyDialog).
#  - Dashboard.tsx: the Register (Show Interest) button opens the gate for that posting type (value->master id
#    via usePostingTypeOptions), onProceed -> registerInterests.

# The NOC wizard's "Program / Category" list was fed by GET /masters?category=posting_type, which for a
# student is filtered by posting-type TARGETING (matchesStudentTargetingForMaster) — so a placed student
# whose posting-type master isn't scoped to their institute/course/branch saw an EMPTY Program list and
# couldn't request a NOC, even though createNoc accepts any active posting type. Now the Program list is
# scoped to how the student was PLACED, per placement source:

#   - Self-Sourced -> ALL ACTIVE posting types (targeting bypassed).
# To pick source before program, the Placement Source radio was moved from step 2 to the TOP of step 1
# (NOC_STEP_FIELDS: step1=['placement_source','program'], step2 loses placement_source; step titles tweaked).
# NO migration. Targeting is unchanged everywhere else (Dashboard interest, Opportunities filter).

#  - masters.schema.ts: queryMastersSchema gains all_targets (z.string -> === 'true'; opt-in, safe boolean).
#  - masters.service.ts: getMasters skips the student-targeting filter when all_targets is set.

#    the query key (masterKeys.publicValues). Backward-compatible (default false).
#  - components/noc/NOCRequestWizard.tsx: postingTypeValuesQuery uses all_targets=true (all-active list for
#    Self-Sourced); new offerPostingTypeValues (placed types) + activePostingTypeValues switch by
#    form.placement_source; Placement Source radio moved into step 1 (resets program on change); step titles.

# system has no email transport; an admin can provision a login later via the existing recruiter flow, which

#    try/catch); guards source==='student' + zero-existing-recruiters + name/email present (idempotent, unique-safe);
#    creates Recruiter (user_id null, verification_status=verified). Wired right after markLinkedCompanyVerified in
#    BOTH tpoApprove and issueNoc. Imports VerificationStatus.

# Bugfix: No Dues (and all) uploaded attachment links open a blank/404 page — "not visible"
#  Root cause: attachments are stored as root-relative `/uploads/...` paths and the shared
#  resolveBackendAssetUrl returned them unchanged, so the browser resolved them against the
#  FRONTEND origin. In a single-origin reverse-proxy prod (frontend + `/api`→backend on one host),
#  only `/api` is forwarded to the backend, so `/uploads/x` 404s. proof_url persist/return/render
#  are all correct — the break is purely file-serving/URL-resolution. Shared resolver → the fix
#  repairs every attachment type (No Dues, NOC, policies, internships, portfolio, resumes,
#  announcements, profile photos). No migration; backward compatible (existing /uploads mount kept).
#  - app.ts: added a second PUBLIC static mount `app.use('/api/uploads', express.static(uploadDir))`
#    placed ABOVE the `/api` auth middleware, so uploads are reachable via the proxied /api path.
#  - studentModule.ts: resolveBackendAssetUrl now prefixes `/uploads/...` paths with the API base
#    (origin + `/api` prefix) instead of returning them relative to the frontend origin; external
#    http(s) URLs and non-upload values are unchanged.
docs/silveroak_backend/src/app.ts
src/lib/studentModule.ts

# Bugfix: Notification module crashed with React error #130 (Element type is undefined)
#  Root cause: NotificationsDropdown's notificationIcons map only covered 4 of the 13
#  NotificationType values, so any modern notification (offer/application/interest/noc/event/
#  announcement/circular/no_dues/recruiter) resolved `Icon` to undefined and `<Icon/>` threw #130,
#  crashing the bell dropdown. Completed the icon map for all 13 types (typed as
#  Record<NotificationType, LucideIcon> so a missing key is now a compile error) and added a
#  `?? Bell` runtime fallback for any unknown/future type. Frontend-only; no backend/migration.

# Bugfix: Notification window can't scroll
#  The shadcn/Radix ScrollArea viewport uses height:100% (h-full), which only resolves against a
#  parent with a definite height; the ScrollArea root had only max-h-[420px] (height:auto), so the
#  viewport grew to full content height and the root clipped it (overflow-hidden) with no scroll.
#  Replaced the ScrollArea with a native `max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto` div, which scrolls
#  correctly with a max-height. Frontend-only.
src/components/layout/NotificationsDropdown.tsx

# Bugfix: Profile photos never rendered in the browser (initials shown everywhere)
#  Uploads persisted and served fine (curl -> 200 image/*), and the read path/types/resolver/avatars
#  were all correct, but NO avatar image rendered in the browser. Root cause: helmet()'s default
#  Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin was applied to the /uploads static responses, so the
#  browser BLOCKED cross-origin <img> loads (dev frontend :8080 embedding images from the API :3000;
#  also any split-origin/CDN prod). curl ignores CORP, which is why the same URL returned 200 on the
#  CLI but showed blank in the app (DevTools: fallback span, no <img>). Fix: serve the two public
#  upload static mounts (/uploads and /api/uploads) with Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: cross-origin
#  via a shared express.static setHeaders handler; the global helmet() default stays strict for every
#  other route. Topology-universal: safe/additive for single-origin prod, and fixes split-origin/CDN.
#  No migration; backend rebuild + restart to ship (CORP is a response header - hard-refresh / purge
#  CDN for /uploads if a stale same-origin header was cached).
docs/silveroak_backend/src/app.ts

# Bugfix: Create Event – Target Audience ignored Company/Role (assigned the whole Posting Type)
#  When creating an event (Posting Type -> Company -> Job Profile/Role), the auto-assigned target
#  audience was every student in the posting type's broad institute/course/branch scope: the backend
#  resolver (event.service.resolvePipelineStudentIds) loaded ALL tenant students and matched them
#  against the event's target_* arrays (seeded from the posting-type master in EventEditorDialog's
#  handlePostingTypeChange), completely ignoring the selected company_id and posting_ids that were
#  already stored on the event. Fix: audience is now the DISTINCT students who APPLIED to the selected
#  role(s) via the Application table (resolveApplicantStudentIds: posting_id IN posting_ids; when no
#  role is selected, fall back to applicants across the company's postings). createEvent/updateEvent
#  now pass company_id + posting_ids and re-resolve (add-only, never removes) when the company or
#  linked role(s) change. Frontend: EventEditorDialog stops seeding target_* from the posting-type
#  master and the "Audience (inherited from Posting Type)" preview (institute/program/branch/semester
#  chips) is replaced with an accurate applicant-based summary keyed off the selected company + roles.
#  Event.target_* columns are now vestigial (kept for back-compat, not dropped). No migration
#  (reuses the existing Application table + Event.company_id/posting_ids).
docs/silveroak_backend/src/modules/events/event.service.ts
src/components/drives/EventEditorDialog.tsx

# Bugfix: Master Data — "Referenced record does not exist" when deleting some posting types
#  Root cause (two layers): (1) Posting.posting_type_master_id is onDelete: Restrict, so Postgres
#  refuses to delete a posting_type master while ANY posting (draft/published/closed) references it —
#  that's why only *some* posting types failed; (2) deleteMaster did no dependency pre-check, so the
#  raw Prisma P2003 hit the generic error-handler branch whose message ("Referenced record does not
#  exist") is worded for the create-with-bad-FK case and is actively misleading for a blocked delete.
#  Fix: deleteMaster now pre-counts postings for posting_type masters and throws ConflictError 409
#  'MASTER_OPTION_IN_USE' naming the count and pointing at the existing Deactivate action. The generic
#  P2003 message was reworded to cover both directions (status 400 + code FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION kept —
#  no contract change). getAdminMasters additionally returns per-posting-type dependent counts
#  (usage{postings,noc_templates,student_preferences,policies}) via 4 groupBy queries scoped to the
#  posting-type ids only (mirrors the existing companies[] pass — deliberately NOT a per-row _count
#  over every master category); the student-facing getMasters passes no counts, so `usage` is
#  admin-only. FE: the delete confirmation now spells out the consequences that were previously
#  silent — NOC templates and student placement-preference rows cascade-delete, linked policies fall
#  back to global — and shows the blocking line when postings exist. Delete button intentionally left
#  ENABLED (server 409 stays the source of truth). Bullets are spans, not <ul>, because
#  AlertDialogDescription renders a <p>. Added 2 backend tests (409 on in-use type; usage present for
#  admin + absent for students). No schema change, NO MIGRATION.

# Bugfix: Announcements — multi-select Institute/Course/Branch + hierarchical Semester targeting
#  Three independent layers were broken: (1) BE Zod capped target_institutes/courses/branches at
#  .max(1), so only ONE of each could ever be saved; (2) the form used PolicyAudienceSelector, three
#  SINGLE SearchableSelects that read targets[0] and write [name]; (3) Semester existed in the DB
#  (Announcement.target_semesters) but was only exposed under the "Specific Semester" audience type
#  and only honoured inside `case 'semester':` of studentMatchesAnnouncement, while institute/course/
#  branch were AND-ed before the switch.
#  Fix — BE: dropped the .max(1) caps; extracted studentMatchesAnnouncementScope() so Institute →
#  Course → Branch → Semester are AND-ed for EVERY audience type (empty level = all); branch now
#  falls back to the parent COURSE (students carry no branch attribute — department is course-derived
#  — so strict branch targeting reached nobody; mirrors matchesStudentTargetingForMaster). New
#  GET /announcements/audience/semesters returns the DISTINCT semesters present among students in the
#  selected scope, with per-semester student counts — there is NO course→semester mapping anywhere
#  (AcademicProfile.semester is a bare Int), so deriving from students is what makes cross-selection
#  (B.Tech + a BCA-only semester) impossible. Route registered ABOVE both GET /:announcementId and the
#  router.use(requireRole('student')) line. Reuses the faculty.service.getStudentFilterOptions pattern
#  and the SAME scope predicate as delivery, so picker and recipients cannot disagree.
#  Fix — FE: new AnnouncementAudienceSelector (4 x SearchableMultiSelect; child options are the UNION
#  over selected parents via useQueries on the existing policyKeys.courses/branches cache keys;
#  invalid child selections are pruned, valid ones kept). PolicyAudienceSelector left UNTOUCHED (the
#  policy dialogs still reference it in commented-out code). target_semesters is now always sent;
#  removed the old "Specific Semester" checkbox block, its required-semester guard, and the now-dead
#  SEMESTER_OPTIONS/toggleSelection/Checkbox. "Specific Semester" removed from the audience dropdown
#  (semester is scope, not a mode); legacy 'semester' announcements load as "All Students" with their
#  semesters preserved — identical recipients, since the scope check now ANDs semesters for all types.
#  No schema change, NO MIGRATION (all four target_* columns already exist as String[]).

# Enhancement: Announcements — republish an archived announcement
#  An archived announcement had NO actions beyond View: the detail-sheet Publish button rendered only
#  for status === 'draft', and publishAnnouncement threw BusinessRuleError 422
#  INVALID_ANNOUNCEMENT_STATUS ("Only draft announcements can be published") for any non-draft status,
#  so archiving was effectively permanent (including a misclick).
#  Fix — BE: NARROWED (not removed) the guard to reject only an already-published announcement, so
#  draft (first publish) and archived (republish) are both valid inputs. The rest of publishAnnouncement
#  was already republish-ready — it sets published_at = now, CLEARS archived_at, re-syncs metrics and
#  notifies students. Kept the 422 + INVALID_ANNOUNCEMENT_STATUS code so the existing "should reject
#  re-publish" test (published -> publish) keeps passing. Swagger updated; added a test that republishes
#  after the existing archive test.
#  Fix — FE: the detail-sheet action renders for status !== 'published' and is labelled "Republish" for
#  archived; the shared ConfirmActionDialog title/description/confirm label, the success toast and the
#  error fallback are all republish-aware. The dialog explicitly says students are notified again —
#  publish broadcasts to ALL tenant students (pre-existing TODO in the service), so repeated republishing
#  would spam the bell. Per decision: students ARE re-notified, published_at IS overwritten (the original
#  first-publish date is lost), and archived announcements stay non-editable. Read/consent receipts are
#  never deleted, so prior read counts carry over — the read rate does not reset.
#  No schema change, NO MIGRATION.

#  printed "____________________" when contact_person_name was null, which is the normal case for a
#  NOC raised from a TPO-released offer (contact person is mandatory only for self_sourced).

#  no-contact-person NOC reads "To," / "Company Name"). The PDF block is laid out from a cursor

#  issued NOCs re-render from their snapshot so they gain the company line on screen, but their
#  already-written PDF files are NOT regenerated (screen and stored file can differ for historical

# Bugfix: Offer Records — pagination controls never rendered (+ server-side search)
#  The Previous/Next controls already existed in OffersManagement.tsx but were dead code. The backend
#  sends pagination in snake_case (paginate() -> {page,limit,total,total_pages,has_next,has_prev})
#  while offerService.getOffers returned that payload RAW, cast to PaginatedOffers whose type declares
#  camelCase totalPages. So pagination.totalPages was undefined at runtime and the render guard
#  `(totalPages ?? 1) > 1` was always false — the pager could never appear and admins were stuck on the
#  first 20 records. TypeScript could not catch it (the response is cast through a generic).
#  Fix: added normalizePagination() to offerService (mirrors the 7 services that already do this —
#  adminService/announcement/circular/internship/noDues/notification/policy), accepting BOTH spellings
#  so a future camelCase API still works; PaginatedOffers gained hasNext/hasPrev for parity.
#  Also (per decision) switched the search box from client-side to SERVER-side: the backend already
#  supported ?search (queryOffersSchema + buildOfferWhere, case-insensitive OR over role / company
#  name / student full_name / enrollment_number) so this was FRONTEND-ONLY — no backend change. The
#  local filteredOffers filter was deleted, `search` is passed from the existing useDeferredValue, and
#  the page resets to 1 on a new search (the 3 Selects already did). ⚠ useOffers gained
#  `placeholderData: keepPreviousData` FIRST — search lives in the query key and the page has an early
#  `if (isLoading) return <skeleton/>`, which would otherwise unmount the search input on every
#  keystroke (same trap already fixed in useUsers and usePostings).
#  Behaviour note: server search matches 4 fields; the old client filter also matched posting.title and
#  posting.type, so searching by posting title/type no longer returns matches (widening it is a backend
#  change, out of the agreed scope). CSV export remains current-page-scoped. FacultyOffers.tsx reads the
#  same pagination.totalPages via the same service, so its pager is fixed too with no change to it.
#  No backend change, no schema change, NO MIGRATION.
src/services/offerService.ts
src/types/offer.ts
src/hooks/use-offer-api.ts
src/pages/admin/OffersManagement.tsx

# Bugfix: NOC wizard — Self-Sourced Program/Category listed EVERY posting type in the tenant
#  ⚠ This REVERSES a deliberate 2026-07-06 decision (flagged to and confirmed by the user): the masters
#  `?all_targets=true` flag was added back then specifically so this dropdown would bypass student
#  targeting. NOCRequestWizard called useMasterValues('posting_type', true, true) and the self_sourced
#  branch returned that unfiltered list verbatim, so a B.Tech student saw BCA-only programmes and vice
#  versa. Expected: only posting types the student is enrolled/eligible for or has shown interest in.
#  Fix (FRONTEND-ONLY, no backend change — every endpoint already existed): the self_sourced list is now
#  the union, deduped by normalizeMasterValue, of (a) ELIGIBLE = useMasterValues('posting_type') WITHOUT
#  all_targets, so the server applies matchesStudentTargetingForMaster — an untargeted posting type still
#  matches everyone (matchesTargetValue returns true on an empty target array), which is what keeps the
#  list non-empty in practice; and (b) INTEREST = useStudentInterests() (GET /students/me/interests — a
#  hook that existed but had NO callers) INTERSECTED with the full active catalogue. The all_targets=true
#  query is kept solely for that intersection, which is required because interest_type is a free string
#  holding legacy values (job, summer_internship, OJT - 2026 HONOURS NEP INTERNSHIP, …) that are no longer
#  posting types — offering one would pass the dropdown then fail the wizard's own "valid posting type
#  from masters" validation at submit. The master's spelling wins so the submitted value is canonical.
#  The UNIVERSITY_DRIVE branch is byte-for-byte unchanged (offer-derived types bypass the filter, so a
#  student placed in an out-of-scope type can still raise their NOC). Validation needed no change — it is
#  already fed postingTypeOptions, so it narrows with the dropdown. Empty list blocks the student with
#  "register interest from your Dashboard first" (per decision: no fallback to all types); `program` was
#  already required so no extra guard was added. postingTypeLoading now covers all three queries.
#  No schema change, NO MIGRATION.
src/components/noc/NOCRequestWizard.tsx

# Bugfix: NOC wizard step 2 — Phone and Pincode had NO format validation
#  Both fields were only LENGTH-capped, front and back: validateNocForm checked pincode <= 10 chars and
#  phone <= 20 chars, and createNocSchema declared z.string().max(10)/max(20). So "abcdefgh" was a
#  valid pincode and "hello" a valid phone — values that then reach the issued certificate and the
#  recruiter auto-created on TPO approval.
#  Fix (per decisions: strict Indian format, fields stay OPTIONAL, validate in BOTH layers):
#  - src/lib/nocModule.ts: added isValidIndianMobile / normalizeIndianMobile / isValidIndianPincode
#    beside the existing isValidPan/isValidGst, following the same "optional field — only validate
#    when the user typed something" contract. Mobile = /^[6-9]\d{9}$/ after normalising (strip
#    separators, drop a leading 91 or 0); pincode = /^[1-9]\d{5}$/ (6 digits, no leading zero).
#  - NOCRequestWizard: the two length checks became format checks; the submit payload now sends
#    normalizeIndianMobile(...) so storage is the bare 10 digits however it was typed; placeholders
#    updated ("+91..." -> "10-digit mobile number", "Pincode" -> "6-digit pincode") with inputMode
#    tel/numeric and maxLength=6 on pincode.
#  - noc.schema.ts: indianMobileSchema + indianPincodeSchema mirror the FE rules. ⚠ refine runs BEFORE
#    transform deliberately — normalising first would reduce "hello" to "" and then accept it as an
#    empty optional value. Both keep '' acceptable for non-form callers. The mobile transform also
#    NORMALISES server-side, so a direct API caller's "+91 98765-43210" is stored as "9876543210".
#  Verified by parsing both layers over a case table: 9876543210 / +91 98765-43210 / 098765 43210 /
#  +919876543210 all accepted and all stored as "9876543210"; 1234567890 (starts 1), 12345, hello, abc,
#  98765 4321 all rejected; omitting both fields still parses (they remain optional).
#  ⚠ Deliberately STRICTER than the rest of the app: student/employer/recruiter/signup schemas use a
#  loose /^\+?[\d\s-]{7,20}$/. This rejects landlines and non-Indian numbers on the NOC contact field.
#  Create-path only — these fields appear in no other schema, so no existing NOC row is re-validated
#  and legacy junk values are left as-is. No schema change, NO MIGRATION.
src/lib/nocModule.ts
src/components/noc/NOCRequestWizard.tsx
docs/silveroak_backend/src/modules/noc/noc.schema.ts

# Bugfix: Offer & Joining Management — Posting Type filter 400'd the whole offers list
#  Selecting any Posting Type produced "Unable to load offers — One or more fields have validation
#  errors." and no rows rendered. Cause: the <SelectItem> bound option.VALUE (the human string, e.g.
#  "Test Placement") but that state is sent as `posting_type_master_id`, which queryOffersSchema
#  declares as z.string().uuid(). Zod rejected it → validate() → 400 VALIDATION_ERROR, whose message
#  is literally "One or more fields have validation errors" (error-handler.ts). Because the table is a
#  single query, a rejected filter takes the entire list down rather than just returning nothing.
#  Verified by parsing queryOffersSchema directly: "Test Placement"/"BCA SPECIAL OJT - 3rd Semester"/
#  "job" all REJECTED "Invalid uuid"; a real master UUID parses OK.
#  Fix: bind option.id (the MasterOption UUID) for both `value` and `key` — the documented id-vs-value
#  trap, already fixed the same way in ApplicationsManagement and PostingsManagement. One line; no
#  other state changed (the 'all' sentinel still maps to undefined, the setPage(1) reset stays).
#  ⚠ Do NOT "consistency sweep" this: SelectionDatabaseTab deliberately binds option.value because its
#  endpoint filters by `posting_type` (the value string), not the UUID. Match the param, not a habit.
#  Same defect verified but LEFT UNFIXED per scope decision (identical one-liners): DrivesManagement
#  (vs event.schema.ts posting_type_master_id uuid) and InternshipsManagement (vs internship.schema.ts).
#  Frontend-only; no backend/schema/migration.
src/pages/admin/OffersManagement.tsx

# Bugfix: Events & Drives → Panel Assignment showed NO students
#  EventAssignmentDialog sourced candidates ONLY from applicants to event.posting_id (the LEGACY single
#  mirror = posting_ids[0]), with the query hard-gated on Boolean(event.posting_id), and then filtered
#  OUT everyone already in event.assigned_students. That empties the list in three separate ways:
#  (1) no linked posting (Linked Posting is optional on the Create Event form) -> query disabled and a
#  dead-end "no live candidate source" message; (2) only the FIRST linked role was queried, so
#  applicants to an event's other roles were invisible; (3) nobody applied — the dev DB's only event
#  has a linked posting with 0 applications and 0 assigned students. Excluding assigned students also
#  meant an existing attendee could never be put on a panel or moved between panels — the actual point
#  of "Panel Assignment".
#  Fix (FRONTEND-ONLY — the API already supported all of it): candidates are now one deduped list of
#  (a) event.assigned_students (already in the event detail payload, which carries panel_id + full
#  student details — no extra request), shown with an "Assigned" badge and a new Panel column;
#  (b) applicants across ALL linked roles via useQueries over event.posting_ids (falling back to
#  [posting_id] for pre-migration events), reusing applicationKeys.listAll + getAllApplications (which
#  already stitches every page); and (c) a fallback to the full student directory (useAdminStudents,
#  server-side search, limit 100) when there is no linked role or no applicants — otherwise assignment
#  is impossible. Assigned entries win the dedupe so their current panel shows. Removed the
#  !event.posting_id dead-end branch; empty/loading states now say WHY the list is empty.
#  assignStudents already UPSERTS with `update: { panel_id }`, so re-submitting an assigned student
#  just moves them — no backend change. student_id from the admin list is Student.id (verified in
#  mapAdminStudent), which is what the assign endpoint expects.
#  ⚠ Behaviour notes: "No panel" now CLEARS an assigned student's panel, so it is relabelled
#  "No panel (remove from panel)"; the directory fallback lets ANY student be assigned, not just
#  applicants; and assignment re-notifies the student (pre-existing notifyManyUsers in assignStudents),
#  which is now reachable when merely moving someone between panels.
#  Note: SlotAllocationDialog.tsx (599 lines, panel+slot per student) is DEAD CODE — no imports
#  anywhere and local-state only — do not "fix" panel assignment there.
#  No schema change, NO MIGRATION.
src/components/drives/EventAssignmentDialog.tsx

# Bugfix: Program-specific (posting-type) policy was silently bypassed
#  The gate modal appeared only occasionally and the API accepted applications regardless. Cause: ONE
#  rule applied in TWO places treated a GLOBAL policy acceptance as acceptance of a posting-type
#  policy — `legacyAcceptedAt && legacyAcceptedAt >= policy.updated_at` where legacyAcceptedAt is
#  Student.policy_accepted_at, which student.service.acceptPolicy writes ONLY for global policies
#  (it explicitly skips the update for linked ones). So any student whose global-acceptance timestamp
#  postdated the linked policy's last edit was auto-exempted: isPolicyAcceptedCurrent returned
#  accepted_current: true -> PostingTypePolicyDialog saw nothing pending and proceeded transparently
#  WITHOUT rendering the modal, and assertPostingTypePolicyAccepted never threw. Whether a student was
#  exempt depended on their timestamp vs the policy's updated_at — hence "only occasionally"; editing
#  the policy bumps updated_at and the gate reappears for a while.
#  Verified on the dev DB BEFORE the fix: 3 linked policies (all on "NEW STIPEND BASED INTERNSHIP
#  PROGRAM -2026 5th B.tech") with 0 explicit acceptances between them; Rahul Sharma (global accepted
#  2026-07-21) was auto-exempted from all three. AFTER: calling the real guard for that student now
#  returns BLOCKED -> POSTING_TYPE_POLICY_NOT_ACCEPTED.
#  Fix (BACKEND ONLY — the frontend already engages whenever accepted_current !== true):
#  - policy.service.ts isPolicyAcceptedCurrent: the legacy fallback now applies ONLY when
#    policy.posting_type_master_id === null (global). Threaded that field through
#    attachStudentAcceptanceStatus's generic (types-only; both callers pass rows straight from
#    prisma.policy.findMany). Affects getPolicies + getPolicyById, which can both return linked policies.
#  - shared/utils/posting-type-policy.ts: dropped the fallback entirely — every policy it loads is
#    linked by construction (queried by posting_type_master_id) — so acceptance must be an explicit
#    PolicyAcceptance row matching the policy's current updated_at. Removed the now-unused
#    policy_accepted_at from the student select.
#  ⚠ DO NOT "tidy" the twin helper hasCurrentAcceptance in student.service.ts — it is identical in
#  shape but its only caller pairs it with buildVisiblePolicyWhere, which is hard-clamped to
#  posting_type_master_id: null, so it governs the GLOBAL gate (dashboard access + the documented
#  placement-gate deadlock). Verified unaffected: a globally-accepted student is NOT re-prompted.
#  Behaviour change: students previously exempt are now prompted once. Do NOT backfill acceptances to
#  "preserve" the old state — that would re-create the bug as data.

# Enhancement: Employer Management -> Create Company — duplicate company prevention via name auto-suggestion.
# Problem: nothing stopped an admin creating a second Company with an existing name. There is NO unique
#  constraint (schema.prisma has only @@index([tenant_id, name])), createCompany was a bare
#  prisma.company.create, and AddCompanyDialog validated only required/length — so "Infosys", "infosys"
#  and "Info-sys" each became separate companies, fragmenting their postings/recruiters/offers.
#  Note the other two create paths ALREADY deduped by name (importCompanies' knownNames set, and NOC's
#  find-or-create with mode:'insensitive'); manual create was the odd one out.
# Fix (two layers, deliberately different strictness):
#  - Server (authoritative): shared/utils/company-name.ts (NEW) — normalizeCompanyName() lowercases and
#    strips everything that is not a letter/digit (punctuation AND all whitespace, so TechCorp / Tech Corp /
#    Tech-Corp share one key; \p{L}\p{N} not [a-z0-9] so non-Latin names don't normalize to '' and bypass
#    the check). findDuplicateCompany(tenantId, name, excludeCompanyId?) compares IN MEMORY — Prisma's
#    mode:'insensitive' covers case but NOT spacing/punctuation, which is the whole point. Wired into
#    createCompany, and into updateCompany (only when data.name is present, always excluding the edited
#    row so an unchanged name can't match itself). Throws ConflictError 409 COMPANY_ALREADY_EXISTS.
#    Deliberately NOT stripping legal suffixes — that would collide "Acme Ltd" with "Acme Inc".
#  - UI (advisory): AddCompanyDialog debounces the name (300ms, local useEffect — there is no shared
#    useDebounce hook; SearchInput's debounceMs prop is declared but never implemented) and queries the
#    existing GET /companies?search. ⚠ It sends buildCompanySearchTerm() = the LONGEST alphanumeric token,
#    not the raw string: the endpoint matches with a raw `contains`, so "Info-sys" would find nothing and
#    the warning would never appear. Exact match -> banner + Save DISABLED; near matches -> advisory list,
#    Save stays ENABLED (per user decision: "Infosys BPM" must not be blocked by "Infosys"). Each shows a
#    "Use this company" button -> navigate('/admin/companies/:id'); both parents already navigate there for
#    row clicks, so NO new props on CompanyManagement/CompanyListTab. 409 is shown on the name field
#    (not a toast) in BOTH dialogs; EditCompanyDialog gets the 409 handling only, no suggestion UI.
#  - useCompanies(params, enabled = true) gained an optional `enabled` flag so the lookup stays idle until
#    >= 2 chars are typed (same pattern as the student list hooks).
#  ⚠ normalizeCompanyName is MIRRORED front and back (no shared package) — keep the two copies in sync.
#  Pre-existing duplicates are NOT touched (dev DB currently has 5 rows named "Test Corp"); the guard only
#  blocks NEW collisions, and renaming one onto another now 409s. No unique index added on purpose: it
#  would fail to apply where duplicates already exist and still wouldn't catch case/spacing variants.
#  No schema change, NO MIGRATION.
docs/silveroak_backend/src/shared/utils/company-name.ts
docs/silveroak_backend/src/modules/employers/employer.service.ts

# Bugfix: Faculty Coordinator -> My Programs listed the whole course cohort under every program
#  (Reported "records not filtered correctly — common records shown instead of only the relevant program
#  records.") studentUnderProgram (faculty.service.ts) included a student when ENROLLED **or** interested,
#  where enrolled = matchesStudentTargetingForMaster(master.target_*, student) — i.e. the student's
#  course/branch merely falls under the posting type's targeting. For a targeted posting type that is the
#  ENTIRE scoped cohort, so every program rendered the same list as the faculty's Student Directory.
#  Proven on the dev DB (replicated the matcher over 8 faculty / 3 active posting types / 32 students):
#  Mayuresh "NEW STIPEND" showed 23 (= whole scope), only 7 had registered interest; Rakesh 12 (=whole);
#  the BCA programs showed all 6 scoped students with 0 interest. The enrolled ∪ interested rule was
#  DOCUMENTED as intentional in CLAUDE.md, so the new definition was confirmed with the user first
#  (Set Instruction 2).
# Fix (per user decision — membership = explicit signal only: interest OR application OR offer):
#  - facultyProgramStudentInclude now also pulls applications + offers, each selecting
#    posting.posting_type_master_id (apps/offers link to a posting type via that real FK, so match on the
#    master ID — no string normalization; interest stays matched by value string since interest_type is a
#    free column with no FK). loadAssignedPostingTypeMasters select gains `id` (+ PostingTypeMaster type).
#  - studentUnderProgram REWRITTEN: dropped the enrolled / hasTargets / matchesStudentTargetingForMaster
#    path entirely. Returns { interested, applied, registered_at }; applied = any application OR offer whose
#    posting.posting_type_master_id === master.id. Inclusion at both call sites (getAssignedPrograms count +
#    getProgramStudents list) is now interested || applied. Source tag: both | interest | applied.
#  - Removed the now-unused matchesStudentTargetingForMaster import (normalizeComparable stays).
#  - The program MAPPING (facultyMatchesPostingTypeMaster — which programs appear, incl. untargeted
#    "Test Placement" mapping to everyone) is UNCHANGED; only student records changed.
#  - FE: FacultyProgramSource 'enrolled' -> 'applied'; sourceLabel + sourceBadge variant updated.
#  Verified post-fix on the dev DB: Mayuresh "NEW STIPEND" 23 -> 8, Rakesh 12 -> 1, BCA programs -> 0
#  (nobody has engaged yet). No schema change, NO MIGRATION.

# Bugfix: Faculty -> Department Students -> View -> Portfolio tab stuck on "Loading..." (never displayed)
#  Two backend bugs on GET /portfolio/:studentId; the FE (PortfolioReadOnlyView + useStudentPortfolio) is fine.
#  (1) portfolio.routes.ts applied a ROUTER-WIDE `router.use(requireRole('student'))` before every route, so a
#      faculty_coordinator hit AuthorizationError ROLE_NOT_ALLOWED (403) and never reached the /:studentId
#      route's OWN requireRole(tpo_admin, tpo_employee, faculty_coordinator, super_admin) — the route-level
#      allowance was dead code. With the default QueryClient (retry=3, backoff) the 403 retried for several
#      seconds = the "stales on loading" spinner. Fix: scope the student-only gate to /me
#      (`router.use('/me', requireRole('student'), requireStudentProfileAccess)`) — every student route lives
#      under /me, and /:studentId (a non-/me path) skips it while keeping its own staff role guard. Dropped the
#      now-unused scopeToDepartment() from the route + its import.
#  (2) Even past the 403, getStudentPortfolio (portfolio.service.ts) scoped faculty by
#      `student.department === scope.department` (scope.department = user.department ?? branches[0]) — the exact,
#      case-sensitive department match the earlier faculty-scope fix already replaced everywhere else. Measured
#      on the dev DB: the exact filter matched 0-1 students where the tolerant Directory shows 6-23, so faculty
#      would 404 on essentially every student. Fix: fetch the student by id+tenant (no department filter; select
#      +course +institute), then for faculty verify studentMatchesFacultyScope(student, resolveFacultyScope(user))
#      — the SAME tolerant matcher as Directory/NOC/My Programs — else NotFound. tpo_admin/tpo_employee/super_admin
#      were already department-filter-free and are unchanged. Dropped the unused `scope` param (controller no
#      longer passes req.scope). Verified with the real shared util: openable set now equals the Directory
#      (8/8/6/11/6/6/12/23), out-of-scope still blocked.
#  Out of scope (flagged, NOT changed): the recruiter Candidate Detail portfolio tab hits the same endpoint but
#  'recruiter' is not in the allowed roles, so it 403s too — a separate access-control decision.
#  No schema change, NO MIGRATION.

# Enhancement: stronger password policy — >=8 chars + >=1 uppercase + >=1 special char (was length-only min(8)).
#  Applies wherever a user SETS a password (student signup, forgot/reset, change-password, admin create-user).
#  LOGIN IS DELIBERATELY UNCHANGED (min(1)) so existing weaker passwords still sign in — no forced reset.
#  Special char = any non-alphanumeric (the requirement's @#$%&* are examples; user-confirmed). One shared
#  validator per side rather than editing regexes in a dozen spots.
# Backend (authoritative):
#  - shared/schemas/common.ts: NEW strongPasswordSchema (min8 + max100 + /[A-Z]/ + /[^A-Za-z0-9]/, ordered
#    messages length->uppercase->special).
#  - auth.schema.ts: completeStudentSignupSchema.password, resetPasswordSchema.new_password,
#    changePasswordSchema.new_password -> strongPasswordSchema; their confirm_* fields dropped to
#    z.string().min(1,'Please confirm your password') (the existing equality superRefine reports mismatches,
#    avoids duplicate uppercase/special errors on the confirm field). loginSchema untouched.
#  - admin.schema.ts: createUserSchema.password -> strongPasswordSchema.
#  - shared/utils/password.ts: generateTemporaryPassword now GUARANTEES >=1 uppercase + >=1 special (from a
#    readable @#$%&* set) at two distinct randomBytes-chosen positions, length clamped >=8 — so a generated
#    temp credential itself satisfies the policy. Still crypto-random, still avoids ambiguous 0/O/1/I/l.
# Frontend (mirror for inline UX; server stays source of truth):
#  - src/lib/passwordPolicy.ts: NEW — PASSWORD_POLICY_HINT, getPasswordPolicyError(pw) (first violated rule,
#    same order as BE), strongPasswordFieldSchema (zod). ⚠ mirrored with the BE strongPasswordSchema — keep in sync.
#  - Replaced inline `password.length < 8` checks with getPasswordPolicyError in ChangePasswordDialog,
#    MyProfile, Login (signup + forgot/reset); ADDED the check to Profile change-password and
#    UserManagementTab admin-create (which only checked non-empty). schemas.ts createUserApiSchema.password ->
#    strongPasswordFieldSchema. Added the PASSWORD_POLICY_HINT helper text under each new-password input and
#    replaced the "Minimum 8 characters" placeholder.
#  Verified: parsed the real BE schemas over a case table (short1!/alllower8!/NoSpecial8 rejected with the
#  specific rule; Strong@123 accepted; mismatch -> "Passwords do not match"; login still accepts any non-empty);
#  generateTemporaryPassword sampled 10x all policy-compliant; FE helper matches BE order. No schema/DB change,
#  NO MIGRATION.

# Bugfix: assigned Faculty Coordinator couldn't see the event under Faculty → Department Events.
# Root cause: the event's coordinator was a FREE-TYPED name string (EventEditorDialog <Input>), but the
# backend scoped a faculty's events by an EXACT name match — applyVisibilityScope (event.service.ts)
# used `where.faculty_coordinators = { has: user.name }`, and markAttendance guard used
# `.includes(user.name)` — so titles/spacing/typos ("Prof. Ramesh Kumar" vs account "Ramesh Kumar")
# never matched → faculty saw nothing. (GET /events already faculty-scopes, so the fetch was fine.)
# Fix (user-confirmed): link the coordinator to a real faculty USER identity and scope by user id.
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED: schema.prisma adds Event.faculty_coordinator_ids String[] @default([]) (additive,
#   legacy-mirror alongside faculty_coordinators names). Run: cd docs/silveroak_backend && npx prisma migrate dev
#   (prisma generate already run; the DB column is added by the migrate.)
# Backend:
#  - event.schema.ts: create schema gains faculty_coordinator_ids: z.array(z.string()).default([]) (updateEvent
#    = createEventSchema.partial() covers it).
#  - event.service.ts: createEvent persists faculty_coordinator_ids (updateEvent spreads {...data});
#    applyVisibilityScope faculty branch → `where.faculty_coordinator_ids = { has: user.id }`; markAttendance
#    guard selects/checks faculty_coordinator_ids.includes(user.id). List/detail use include: (all scalars), so
#    the field flows to the FE automatically.
# Frontend:
#  - types/event.ts: CreateEventInput + ApiEventListItem + ApiEventDetail gain faculty_coordinator_ids: string[].
#  - eventModule.ts: EventFormValues.faculty_coordinators(string) → faculty_coordinator_ids(string[]);
#    buildCreateEventPayload(values, coordinatorNames=[]) sends ids + names; eventDetailToFormValues prefills ids.
#  - EventEditorDialog.tsx: the free-text Faculty Coordinators <Input> replaced by a SearchableMultiSelect of
#    real faculty accounts (useUsers({role:'faculty_coordinator',is_active:'true'}) → value=id,label=name);
#    handleSubmit resolves the selected ids → names and passes both to the payload.
#  - schemas.ts: eventFormSchema gains faculty_coordinator_ids.
# ⚠ Legacy events (free-typed names, no ids) still won't appear for faculty until an admin re-opens the event and
#   re-selects the coordinator(s) — no backfill (names can't be reliably mapped to user accounts).
# BE tsc 0, FE tsc 91 (baseline). User runs: npx prisma migrate dev.

# Bugfix: Faculty → Department Students export failed with an "export limit" error
# Root cause: the export did a SINGLE fetch with limit:5000, but the backend caps the pagination limit
#   at 100 (paginationSchema.max(100)), so Zod rejected the request → "Unable to export student list."
# Fix (FE-only, no backend change, no migration): handleExport now PAGES THROUGH the list endpoint at
#   limit:100 (the backend max) and accumulates every matching student. Page count is derived from the
#   reliable pagination.total (facultyService pagination is snake/camel-broken — totalPages is undefined
#   at runtime, so it must NOT be read); a runaway guard breaks if a page returns 0 rows.
#   EXPORT_LIMIT(5000) → EXPORT_PAGE_SIZE(100). All matching rows now export, filter-scoped.
# Note: the Date Range filter was ALREADY present (dateRange → sharedFilters → both list + export) and
#   already applies to the export — no code change needed for it, only verification.
# FE tsc 91 (baseline, zero net-new).
src/pages/faculty/DepartmentStudents.tsx

# Enhancement: Column sorting on management tables — PHASE 1 (server-side, admin + superadmin + faculty)
# Adds clickable column-header sorting to ~20 high-traffic management list tables. Server-side: a header click
#   sends sort_by/sort_order to the API so the WHOLE dataset is sorted across pages (not just the visible page),
#   and pagination resets to page 1.
# Shared FE primitives (NEW):
#  - src/components/shared/SortableTableHead.tsx — clickable <TableHead> (neutral ArrowUpDown → ArrowUp/ArrowDown),
#    className passthrough for text-right/hidden/width, aria-sort.
#  - src/hooks/use-server-sort.ts — useServerSort<TKey>(default, dir, onChange) two-state toggle; onChange resets page.
# Backend: per-module getXOrderBy(sort_by, sort_order) WHITELISTS (mirror faculty getStudentOrderBy) replace the raw
#   `orderBy: { [sort_by || default]: sort_order }` (which injected sort_by into Prisma unvalidated) — closes that
#   Prisma-injection/runtime-error surface. Relational columns → nested orderBy ({ student:{full_name} }, { company:{name} }),
#   Drives Panels/Students → { panels/assigned_students: { _count } }, posting type → { posting_type_master:{value} }.
#   Added Prisma namespace import where missing. NO migration (only orderBy logic).
# FE sort_by union types tightened from string → exact whitelist unions (mirror BE) so tsc rejects a non-whitelisted
#   column; useServerSort<TKey> parameterized to the same union per page.
# Deferred to Phase 2 (endpoints not server-sortable / not server-paginated): PortfolioMonitoringTab, InterestLists,
#   FacultyPrograms, EligibleStudentsTab, SelectionDatabaseTab, CircularsManagement/FacultyCirculars, and the 32 reports.
#   Excluded: RolesPermissionsTab (role×module matrix). Non-sortable columns (Actions, Compensation, Compliance, Mock
#   Result, Posting Types, Audience, Application Window, CGPA, etc.) render plain <TableHead>.
# Verified: BE tsc 0, FE tsc 91 (baseline, zero net-new).

# Enhancement: NOC Internship Completion Certificate (submit → TPO approve → portfolio + Issued consolidation)
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED — schema.prisma: new enum CompletionCertStatus { pending, approved, rejected } +
#   NocRequest columns completion_certificate_url/_name/_mime_type/_size, completion_status,
#   completion_submitted_at, completion_reviewed_by/_by_name/_at, completion_remarks, completion_due_notified_at.
#   All additive/nullable (auto-migratable, no backfill). Run: cd docs/silveroak_backend && npx prisma migrate dev
#   (npx prisma generate already run). Restart BE.
# Completion tracking is a SEPARATE lifecycle from NOC issuance — the NOC stays `issued`; completion_status
#   tracks the uploaded certificate (null=not submitted → pending → approved/rejected).
# Student: on the Completed tab, issued NOCs show an "Upload Completion Certificate" button (re-upload when
#   rejected) + a status badge + a "Completion Certificate" view link. Lazy end-date reminder (no cron exists):
#   getMyNocs fires a one-time notification per issued NOC whose end_date has passed with no cert submitted
#   (completion_due_notified_at idempotency).
# TPO: new "Completion Certificates" tab (pending list) → CompletionReviewDialog Approve/Reject (mandatory
#   remark on reject; student may re-upload → Pending). Approval creates a student Certification (portfolio) +
#   recalcProfileCompletion (best-effort, idempotent by document_url) and notifies the student. Every
#   submit/approve/reject is written to the audit log (history). Issued tab consolidates per student: Offer
#   Letter + NOC Certificate + Completion Certificate links + completion status/approval-date/remarks.
# Endpoints (noc.routes): POST /noc/:nocId/completion-certificate (student, multer, upload+attach),
#   PUT /noc/:nocId/completion-certificate/approve|reject (tpo, noc_requests:approve). getNocs gains a
#   completion_status filter. Verified: BE tsc 0, FE tsc 91 (baseline).

# Enhancement: Student No Dues → Request No Dues Certificate — dynamic form per Exit Reason
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED: schema.prisma → model NoDuesRequest gains 7 nullable columns
#   (sou_passing_year, company_sector, company_address, language_test, university_address,
#   examination_name, additional_details). Run: cd docs/silveroak_backend && npx prisma migrate dev
# Exit Reason is now a dropdown (5 options) driving a fully dynamic field set + per-reason proof helper text:
#   Job/Employment (passing year, company, designation, sector, package CTC, company address, joining date),
#   Business (passing year, business name/sector/address), Planning-for-Further-Studies (passing year,
#   country, intended course=program_name, preferred university=institution_name, language test — required

# Bugfix: University-drive NOCs showed blank Recruiter Name/Contact/Email/Designation (record + report)
# Root cause: the NOC wizard's university_drive (offer) branch prefills only company_name + role_title —
#   it never collects contact_person_*, so those columns persist null. All record views
#   (NOCRequestDetailSheet/NOCReviewDialog) read contact_person_*, and the Internship-NOC listing report
#   maps recruiter_* = contact_person_*, so everything rendered blank. The offer is not persisted on the
#   NOC; the only durable link to a recruiter is NocRequest.company_id -> Company.recruiters[].
# Fix (BACKEND-ONLY, no migration, no FE change): recover recruiter details from the linked company.
#   noc.service.ts: new exported helpers pickCompanyRecruiter() (prefers a verified recruiter, else
#     earliest-created) + contactWithRecruiterFallback() (fills the four contact_person_* from the company
#     recruiter, fill-empty only) + NOC_COMPANY_RECRUITER_SELECT.
#   (A) createNoc: when contact_person_* are empty, look up the company's recruiter and denormalize
#       name/phone/designation/email into the columns -> new university-drive NOCs carry recruiter details
#       durably (record + report + issued certificate snapshot).
#   (B) Read fallback for existing rows: getMyNocs/getNocById/getNocs now include company.recruiters and
#       coalesce via contactWithRecruiterFallback (both the faculty in-memory branch and the normal branch).
#   report.analytics.service.ts: getInternshipNocListingReport selects company.recruiters and coalesces
#       recruiter_* = contact_person_* || pickCompanyRecruiter(...) (imports the helper from noc.service).
# Self-sourced NOCs unchanged (their own contact values win). Degrades gracefully to null when the company
#   has no recruiter (data gap, not a bug). Verified: BE tsc 0; helper unit-checks pass; 7 companies have
#   recruiters on the dev DB. No FE change (displays read contact_person_*, report reads recruiter_*).
backend/src/modules/noc/noc.service.ts
backend/src/modules/reports/report.analytics.service.ts

# This screen is a faceted discovery UI — 6 of 7 filters (work mode/locations/domains/stipend/ctc/type-by-value),

# Enhancement (Master Data -> Posting Types: cascading Institute->Course->Branch->Semester ROW table): the posting-type
# targeting scope was four INDEPENDENT multi-selects that the matcher ANDs (a cross-product — couldn't express
# "SOCET/B.Tech/CE/7" separately from "SOM/MBA/-/4"). Replaced with a dependent, cascading ROW table (invoice-style, per
# the mockup): each row is one complete Institute+Course+Branch+Semester combination; a student matching ANY row is
# eligible (OR of rows). All four cells required per row; Academic Years stays a separate selector.
# Data: new MasterOption.scope_rows Json @default("[]") holding [{institute,course,branch,semesters[]}] (additive,
# grandfathers existing rows). The flat target_* arrays are KEPT as a legacy fallback AND auto-derived as the UNION of the
# rows on save (so faculty "My Programs" mapping + event pipeline inheritance keep working). Matching: new
# matchesScopeRows(rows, student, {branchFallback}) in shared/utils/student-targeting.ts — used when scope_rows present in
# BOTH matchesStudentTargeting (branchFallback false) and matchesStudentTargetingForMaster (branchFallback true, since
# students carry no real branch); else the existing flat-array AND. Backend: masters.schema (scopeRowSchema on
# create/update + refine), masters.service (parseScopeRows/normalizeScopeRows/deriveFlatTargetsFromRows; mapMasterOption
# emits scope_rows for posting_type only; create/update write scope_rows + derive flat union when non-empty; update leaves
# flat arrays untouched when rows empty so a rename doesn't wipe legacy targeting), report.analytics selects scope_rows on
# its two posting-type-master queries. Frontend: new ScopeRowTable.tsx (bordered table, green "+" header add-button;
# per-row ScopeRowEditor child owning its own course/branch cascade queries — Institute->Course->Branch SearchableSelects
# + Semester multi-select + red trash delete) and PostingTypeScopeEditor.tsx (wraps the table + separate Academic Years
# multi-select); MasterDataManagement uses PostingTypeScopeEditor in BOTH create + edit (replacing UserScopeSelector +

# Enhancement (NOC Re-Issued Request — versioned NOC records + Active-only reports): students who change
# companies can re-issue an existing NOC. New "Request for Re-Issued NOC" checkbox on the create wizard (Step 1)
# reveals: Previous NOC dropdown (ISSUED NOCs only), Reason for Company Change, Termination/Relieving Letter (PDF);
# all required when checked. Submitting creates a NEW NocRequest that SHARES the previous NOC's reference number,
# becomes the ACTIVE record, and marks all prior records with that number INACTIVE (only the latest is active). Max
# 4 re-issues per number (5th blocked, NOC_REISSUE_LIMIT_REACHED). Rejecting an active re-issue auto-reverts the
# previous record to Active. All versions retained for audit. Lists (student card / detail sheet / review dialog /
# admin + faculty tables) show ALL versions with an Active/Inactive + Re-Issued badge and the re-issue details +
# termination-letter link. ALL NOC reports (5 dedicated + 3 placement NOC sub-queries) now filter is_active:true so
# listing + counters + export show ONLY active NOCs.
# Schema: dropped @unique on noc_number (re-issues share it); added is_active, is_reissue, previous_noc_id (self-
# relation NocReissue), reason_for_company_change, termination_letter_url/name + indexes. Issue-time sequence now
# counts DISTINCT noc_number + a collision loop (no more @unique/P2002 reliance); a re-issue keeps its inherited
# number at issue. New PDF-only POST /noc/termination-letter upload endpoint. BE tsc 0, FE tsc 87 (baseline).

# Bugfix: TPO Admin (+ faculty) date-range filters didn't actually filter. TWO root causes. NO migration.
# (1) Interest table (admin.service.getInterestRegistrations) filtered the matching registration on a
# NON-EXISTENT column `created_at` inside interest_registrations.some({...}) — InterestRegistration's
# timestamp column is `registered_at` (the invalid field slipped past TS via a conditional object spread).
# Fixed the key to `registered_at`. (2) All AdminListScopeFilters consumers serialized the picked local-
# midnight Date with `.toISOString()`, shifting it to the previous UTC calendar day for +offset zones (IST);
# combined with the backend's server-local `end.setHours(23,59,59,999)` this collapsed a same-day filter to
# a ~5.5h window on the wrong day -> "no results". Fix: new shared `toDateParam(date)` in lib/formatters.ts
# returns local `yyyy-MM-dd`; every consumer now sends date-only strings. buildDateRangeCondition
# (shared/utils/student-scope-filter.ts) now treats a `yyyy-MM-dd` string as an explicit UTC calendar day
# (T00:00:00.000Z .. T23:59:59.999Z), server-TZ-independent; full-ISO/Date callers keep the legacy path
# (backward compatible). Reports (separate parseDateRange path) untouched. BE tsc 0, FE tsc 67 (baseline).

backend/src/shared/utils/student-scope-filter.ts

# Bugfix: Issued/downloaded NOC PDF — body text OVERLAPPED the student-detail rows and the signature block
# for long template bodies. The PDF renderer positioned the body with a downward flow from y=558 but PINNED
# the student block at y=300 and the signature at y=198 (fixed absolute-Y), so a long body (e.g. the reported
# template with a duplicated paragraph) flowed past y=300 and drew on top of them. The on-screen preview
# (NocTemplatePreview.tsx) is CSS flex-flow so it never overlapped — hence "shows while editing but not in the
# issued NOC". Fix (PDF renderer only): FLOW the student block below the actual body end and the signature
# below the student block, via a Math.min hybrid — studentBlockTop = min(300, bodyEndY - 10),
# withRegardsY = max(168, min(198, studentBlockBottom - 18)) with the signature's internal offsets (stamp -44,
# name -52, title -66, email -78) kept relative to withRegardsY; the 168 floor keeps the signature clear of the
# pinned letterhead footer. Short/normal bodies keep the exact current layout (min() returns the old fixed y),
# so no regression; long bodies flow down and no longer overlap. Removed the now-unused NOC_SIGNATURE_STAMP_Y/
# NAME_Y/TITLE_Y/EMAIL_Y module consts (replaced by relative locals). Verified by rendering the reported
# long-body template + a short body through the real generateNocCertificatePdf: long body no longer overlaps,
# short body pixel-identical to before. Note: issued PDFs are never regenerated, so only NOCs issued AFTER
# deploy get the fix. No preview change, no schema, no migration. BE tsc 0.

# Enhancement: PlaceReady — Phase 0c: placeready-mobile Flutter student app scaffold (NEW app, no backend change).
# `flutter create` app at placeready-mobile/ (org ac.in.silveroakuni, android+ios). Shares the portal backend
# over bearer-JWT. lib/core/api_client.dart = Dio client with an auth interceptor that attaches the bearer token
# and does a single-flight 401 refresh + one retry (mirrors the web apiClient); lib/core/token_store.dart keeps
# tokens in flutter_secure_storage; lib/core/auth.dart = AuthController (Provider/ChangeNotifier) with bootstrap-
# from-stored-token, login, logout; lib/models/auth_user.dart parses user+permissions (.can(module,action),
# super_admin bypass). Screens: login + home shell (Today/Drives/Progress/Profile tabs, placeholder). Config via
# --dart-define API_BASE_URL/TENANT_SLUG (Android emulator uses 10.0.2.2; usesCleartextTraffic=true for dev http).
# Deps added: dio, flutter_secure_storage, provider. Verified: flutter pub get, flutter analyze (no issues),
# flutter test (pass). Real student journey + OpenAPI-generated Dart models come in Phase 4.
placeready-mobile/ (new Flutter app: pubspec.yaml, lib/*, android manifest cleartext, test/widget_test.dart)

# Enhancement: PlaceReady — Phase 1a: content-foundation schema slice + catalog + seed.
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED: schema.prisma gained 6 pr_* models — PrTestType (pr_test_types), PrQuestionBank
# (pr_question_banks), PrQuestion (pr_questions), PrVideoCategory (pr_video_categories), PrVideoSubCategory
# (pr_video_subcategories), PrPracticeVideo (pr_practice_videos) — plus 4 onDelete:Cascade back-relations on
# the Tenant model (pr_test_types/pr_question_banks/pr_video_categories/pr_practice_videos). test_type_id on a
# bank is onDelete:Restrict (app-guarded); questions cascade via bank; subcategories via category; videos'
# category/subcategory FKs are SetNull. Difficulty/evaluation/source are String cols (Zod-validated), not enums.
# Run: cd backend && npx prisma migrate dev --name placeready_phase1  (npx prisma generate already run).
# Also: question-type catalog CONSTANT (src/modules/pr-question-banks/question-type-catalog.ts, ~26 types with
# family+evaluation auto/ai/manual + difficulty list) — served as reference, not a table; standard test-type
# list (src/modules/pr-test-types/standard-test-types.ts) seeded in prisma/seed.ts (fresh DB) and lazily
# per-tenant in 1b. BE tsc 0.

# Enhancement: PlaceReady — Phase 1b: content-foundation backend modules (needs the 1a migration applied to run).
# Three new modules (5-file pattern) mounted after the /api auth+tenant gate in app.ts:
#  - pr-test-types  -> /api/pr/test-types : list (lazy-seeds standard types per tenant via
#    ensureTenantPlaceReadySeeded), create, update, PATCH /:id/active, DELETE /:id (409 PR_TEST_TYPE_IN_USE
#    when a bank references it). requirePermission('pr_test_types', ...).
#  - pr-question-banks -> mounted at /api/pr : GET /question-types (serves the code catalog), bank CRUD
#    (/question-banks[/:id]), questions (/question-banks/:id/questions, PUT|DELETE /questions/:questionId),
#    and POST /question-banks/:id/bulk-import (reuses createQuestionSchema per row, createMany). Question
#    text/type/difficulty/options/correct validated; question_type_key checked against the catalog.
#    requirePermission('pr_question_banks', ...).
#  - pr-videos -> /api/pr/videos : category + subcategory CRUD and video CRUD with category/subcategory/
#    search/active filters; unique-name conflicts -> 409. requirePermission('pr_practice_videos', ...).
# Mounts ordered so /api/pr/test-types and /api/pr/videos resolve before the catch-all /api/pr bank router.
# Minimal @openapi swagger tag files added per module. BE tsc 0. (Runtime needs `prisma migrate dev` from 1a.)
backend/src/app.ts
backend/src/modules/pr-test-types/pr-test-type.{schema,service,controller,routes,swagger}.ts
backend/src/modules/pr-question-banks/pr-question-bank.{schema,service,controller,routes,swagger}.ts
backend/src/modules/pr-videos/pr-video.{schema,service,controller,routes,swagger}.ts
