# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED (two DBs): PlaceReady extracted into a standalone backend service.
# Enhancement: PlaceReady backend is now a SEPARATE service (placeready-backend/) with its OWN
#   database, trusting SOU-issued HS256 JWTs (shared JWT_SECRET, verify-only, no user store), static
#   role-based permissions (ROLE_MODULE_DEFAULTS, no RolePermission table), and a souClient that
#   forwards the caller's bearer token to the SOU backend for shared reads (events, admin students,
#   portfolio). All 5 pr-* modules (test-types, question-banks, videos, drives, assessments) + the 20
#   pr_ Prisma models MOVED from ./backend into placeready-backend/. pr-drives' SOU-table reads
#   (event/student/academicProfile) converted to souClient HTTP calls. Mounted under /api/pr/* on :4000.
#   SOU cleanup: removed the 5 pr-* modules, their app.ts imports+mounts, the 20 pr_ models +
#   Tenant reverse relations from schema.prisma, all pr_ entries from permissions.ts, and the
#   PlaceReady seed block. Both typecheck clean (PR tsc 0, SOU tsc 0).
# USER STEPS: (1) cd placeready-backend && npm install ; (2) create the `placeready` Postgres DB ;
#   (3) cd placeready-backend && npx prisma migrate dev --name init_placeready (creates pr_ tables) ;
#   (4) cd backend && npx prisma migrate dev --name drop_pr_tables (drops the moved pr_ tables — dev
#   data re-seeded). placeready-backend/.env carries the SOU JWT_SECRET + a separate DATABASE_URL.
# --- new service (representative paths; whole placeready-backend/ tree is new) ---

# Enhancement: PlaceReady Mobile phase — wire the Flutter student graded journey to the live
#   PlaceReady backend (:4100) + student-scoping/hardening. (2026-09-30, NO migration.)
#   BACKEND (placeready-backend):
#   - Unify id space on the SOU Student.id: new resolveStudentId(token) (sou-client.ts, via the fixed
#     souGetMyProfile → GET /students/me → student.id). Drive registrations + stage results are keyed on
#     Student.id, so attempts must be too. pr-assessments controller now resolves Student.id for
#     start/submit (was req.user.id). On submit, when the assessment is a drive module, an upsert of
#     PrStageResult (status passed/completed/failed, score=pct) is added to the transaction so the drive
#     funnel advances.
#   - NEW student-scoped drive reads: GET /pr/drives/mine (registered drives + per-stage progress) and
#     GET /pr/drives/:id/mine (one drive: stages + status + assessment_id per module + gate/lock), both
#     deriving Student.id from the token, never souGetAdminStudent. Registered before /:id so "mine"
#     isn't parsed as a uuid.
#   - Permission hardening: students lose pr_assessments:create + pr_drive_tests:create (view only);
#     take-attempt routes re-gated create→view; all STAFF drive routes now require staff roles
#     (requireRole), so students can't read tenant-wide drive data — they use /mine only.
#   - seed.ts: optional PR_SEED_STUDENT_ID registers the demo student to the drives; added a TCS
#     "Aptitude" assessment (auto-scored) so the funnel has a launchable stage.
#   MOBILE (placeready-mobile):
#   - config.dart PR_BASE_URL; api_client.dart parameterized baseUrl (refresh stays pinned to SOU auth
#     base); auth.dart adds a second ApiClient `prApi` sharing the token store.
#   - NEW lib/core/pr_service.dart: typed /pr/* client + JSON→model mappers (assessment definition →
#     AsmMetaX/AItem; DTOs for drives/funnel/attempt-result/videos). Normalizes int-keyed capture maps
#     (match/assign/matrix) to string keys so JSON encoding never throws.
#   - Wired screens: drives_screen (myDrives), drive_detail_screen (myDrive funnel; assessment-linked
#     stages launch the live player, others fall back to practice screens), assessment_player_screen
#     (live mode: getDefinition→startAttempt→submit→server-scored result; fixture mode preserved),
#     practice_videos_screen (live videos + categories). Imperative setState-bool + SnackBar loading/error.
#   Backend tsc 0, flutter analyze clean, PR /pr/drives/mine mounted (401 w/o token). NO migration.
# USER STEPS: (1) restart placeready-backend (tsx watch usually auto-reloads) ;
#   (2) PR_SEED_TENANT_ID=<tenant> PR_SEED_STUDENT_ID=<SOU Student.id of student@silveroak> npm run db:seed ;
#   (3) flutter run --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:3000/api
#       --dart-define=PR_BASE_URL=http://localhost:4100/api (Chrome; emulator uses 10.0.2.2). SOU CORS

# Enhancement: PlaceReady — Question Bank per-question-type authoring UI
# In TPO Admin → Content → Question Banks, the Add/Edit-question panel didn't adapt to the selected
# question type: it showed a choice-options editor for single/multi families and a single "reference
# answer" textarea for everything else — so True/False, Fill-in-the-blank, Numerical, Coding, SQL,
# Matching, Ordering, Reading-comprehension, Rating, Upload and Audio all looked the same. Rebuilt
# QuestionDraftPanel with a per-family editor (renderEditor) covering all 26 catalog types across 12
# families: fixed True/False & Yes/No option rows, answer-key input for short/fill/numerical/integer,
# left↔right pair editor for match, reorderable list for ordering, language+starter+tests for coding
# (SQL variant), passage+sub-question for comprehension, scale for likert, kind/hint for upload,
# recording limit for audio/video; plus a Marks input (previously non-editable) and per-family save
# validation. Answer key / render config is stored in the existing generic PrQuestion.correct JSON
# column (already round-trips via z.any()), so this is FE-only — NO backend change, NO migration. The
# saved-question list row now shows a per-family summary. Field names mirror the assessment builder /
# mobile player config so a bank question can seed an assessment later.
placeready-web/src/pages/content/QuestionBanksTab.tsx


# Enhancement: PlaceReady — Question Bank Excel bulk upload for ALL question types
# The Bulk-upload panel + questionImport.ts were choice-only (Question, Option A–D, Correct, Difficulty,
# Type) and ignored marks / answer keys / family config, so the newer question types couldn't be
# uploaded. Reworked the importer to an 8-column template — Question, Question Type, Difficulty, Marks,
# Options, Correct, Reference/Extra, Language — with a '|' delimiter, and a family-aware parser that emits
# the SAME QuestionInput shape the manual editor's buildBody produces (options / correct JSON /
# reference_answer / marks) for every family: choice, true/false, yes/no, short/fill/numerical/integer
# (answer keys), long/essay (rubric), match (Left=Right pairs), ordering (items in order), coding/sql
# (language + starter), reading-comprehension (passage + sub-question), rating (scale + labels), upload
# (kind + instructions), audio/video (limit). The on-portal "Download template" button now yields a
# two-sheet .xlsx: a Questions sheet with one filled example per type + an attached Guide sheet explaining
# every column per type. Per-row validation mirrors the editor's guards. Preview + saved-list summaries
# reuse a generalized summarize(). FE-only — the backend bulkImport already accepts the rich rows via
# z.any() correct. NO backend change, NO migration.
placeready-web/src/lib/questionImport.ts

# Enhancement: PlaceReady Phase 7 — Admin Governance (config · audit · data deletion · read-only users/roles)
# Super-admin Admin console made real end-to-end (was 6 mock pages): DB-backed System Config (feature flags),
# immutable Audit Log, full Data-Deletion chain (student requests on mobile → TPO Head approves → super-admin
# executes a 10-table irreversible erase + privileged audit), read-only Users/Roles proxied live from SOU
# (/admin/users via forwarded token), and a live Admin Dashboard. Enforces the notifications_send_enabled flag
# in nudge send (other 3 flags stored + advisory). Readiness weights stay code constants (operator wall).
#
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED: prisma/schema.prisma adds 3 models — PrSetting, PrAuditLog, PrDeletionRequest
#   (tables pr_settings / pr_audit_logs / pr_deletion_requests). Run:
#     cd placeready/backend && npx prisma migrate dev --name pr_admin_governance
#   then restart the PR service.

# psychometric/…) run through the existing live assessment player, plus a live Progress tab
# (readiness ring + pillars + practice history). Reuses the generic assessment engine — a
# drive_id:null attempt is fully scored but never writes a PrStageResult, so practice stays OUT

# "coming soon" card). NO migration — all existing models reused.

#   grouped by module_key category), GET /pr/assessments/attempts/mine (practice history),

# Enhancement: Repoint PlaceReady web console auth to the independent PlaceReady backend (:4100).

# (= PR_BASE_URL); VITE_API_BASE_URL moved from :3000 (SOU) to :4100 (PlaceReady) so both clients
# share the PlaceReady origin. Two SOU-only reads (/events, /admin/students in souSharedService)
# now resolve to PlaceReady which doesn't own them — left in place with TODO(independent-auth)

# Enhancement: PlaceReady independent auth (SilverOak-exact) + CRM-direct + SOU service-account for
# drives. PlaceReady now owns its identities instead of forwarding SOU tokens. Auth model matches
# SilverOak exactly: one-time STUDENT SIGNUP (enrollment → OTP to CRM official email → set password)
# + routine email/password LOGIN; staff are SEEDED (super_admin/tpo_admin/tpo_employee/
# faculty_coordinator/management, password Password@123). PlaceReady issues its own HS256 JWTs (own
# JWT_SECRET; 15m access + DB-persisted rotating refresh tokens w/ reuse-detection). New Prisma models
# Tenant/User/RefreshToken/Student (pr_tenants/pr_users/pr_refresh_tokens/pr_students); permissions are
# CODE-DEFINED (shared/permissions.ts buildPermissionsForRole — no RolePermission table). CRM called
# DIRECTLY (crm-client.ts, Api-Key header: GetStudentDetailByEnrollmentNo / GetInstituteList /
# GetCourseList / GetBranchList). SOU is reached only via a SERVICE ACCOUNT (sou-client.ts lazily logs
# in with SOU_SERVICE_EMAIL/PASSWORD, caches the bearer, re-logins on 401) for campus-drive events.
# OTP email via ZeptoMail (shared/utils/email.ts; dev fallback STUDENT_SIGNUP_OTP=000000 when unconfigured).
# authenticate middleware verifies the PR token + DB-loads the live User (rejects inactive) and attaches
# student_id for students. Mobile: login=email+password + "New student? Register" signup flow; profile +
# all student reads keyed on req.user.student_id; /pr/students/me serves the local Student row.
# FINAL FIX (this change): the two TPO Drives web reads that broke when the web client repointed to
# :4100 now have PlaceReady-owned backends — NEW module src/modules/pr-sou/ (staff-gated):
#   GET /api/pr/sou/events   — proxies souGetEvents(query) via the service account ({data,pagination}).
#   GET /api/pr/sou/students — local prisma.student.findMany scoped to req.user.tenant_id, search over
#                              name/enrollment, paginated, mapped to the web SouStudent shape.
# Web souSharedService.listEvents/listAdminStudents repointed to /pr/sou/events|/pr/sou/students on the
# PlaceReady apiClient (TODO(independent-auth) markers + the now-unused souApiClient import removed).
# Verified: backend tsc 0, web tsc 0 + build clean, mobile flutter analyze clean.
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED: prisma/schema.prisma adds models Tenant (pr_tenants), User (pr_users),
#   RefreshToken (pr_refresh_tokens), Student (pr_students). Run in placeready/backend:
#   `npx prisma migrate dev --name pr_identity` then seed `npx tsx prisma/seed.ts`.
#   ⚠ Pr*.student_id now means the LOCAL Student.id, so pre-existing dev Pr* rows orphan.
# ⚠ OPS (gitignored placeready/backend/.env only): set real CRM_API_KEY, MAIL_ZEPTO_TOKEN,
#   SOU_SERVICE_EMAIL + SOU_SERVICE_PASSWORD. Restart :4100 after .env changes.

# Enhancement: PlaceReady web TPO Drives — active-only list + full drive-detail Overview card.
# (1) The Drives list now shows ONLY active drives (status Published or Registration open); Draft &
# Closed are hidden. Implemented client-side in DriveList: `ACTIVE_DRIVE_STATUSES` filter over the
# fetched rows (status dropdown trimmed to All active / Published / Registration open). (2) The drive
# DETAIL page gains an Overview summary card (between the stat cards and the tabs) showing company,
# role, CTC band, stipend, duration, min CGPA, max backlogs, final threshold, eligible branches,
# location, skills, and the enrolled-students count (+ a "View students" button that jumps to the
# existing Students matrix tab). CTC/eligibility are not stored on the drive or exposed by SOU's events
# API, so a NEW best-effort backend endpoint resolves them from the linked SOU posting:
#   GET /api/pr/drives/:id/overview  (staff-gated, requirePermission(pr_drive_tests,'view'))
# → service `getDriveOverview`: drive.sou_event_id → souGetEvent → posting_id/posting_ids[0] →
#   souGetPosting (new helper in sou-client.ts; SOU postings API returns ctc/stipend/duration/
#   bond_details/min_cgpa/max_backlogs/eligible_branches/target_branches/skill_requirements/location,
#   callable by the existing tpo_admin service account). All SOU calls are wrapped in try/catch — any
#   failure (or a drive with no sou_event_id) returns sou_available:false + the drive's own stored
#   fallbacks (jd.ctc, cgpa_min, `eligible` label), so the card always renders. NO SOU change.
# Enrolled-students LIST reuses the existing GET /:id/students matrix tab (no new student endpoint).
# Verified: backend tsc 0; web tsc 0 + build clean; new route resolves (random id → PR_DRIVE_NOT_FOUND).
# NO schema change, NO migration. ⚠ Dev-data note (not a code issue): the 2 existing demo drives are
# orphaned under the pre-identity-migration tenant, so they don't appear under the seeded
# silver-oak-university tenant until a drive is created there (or they're re-pointed).

# Bugfix: PlaceReady Drives — students must come from SOU, not the empty local pr_students table.
# The independent-auth pass had repointed BOTH the drive audience pool and the drive student-hydration
# to the local Student table, which only holds mobile-app signups (0 rows) — so the New-drive audience
# selector was empty and assigned students couldn't be named. Restored the pre-migration design: both now
# read the SOU roster via the service account (tpo_admin), which is the authoritative student source.
#   - sou-client.ts: added souGetAdminStudents(query) → GET /admin/students, and souGetAdminStudent(id)
#     → GET /admin/students/:id (both service-account auth).
#   - pr-sou.service.ts `listStudents`: now proxies souGetAdminStudents({page,limit,search}) and passes
#     through SOU's {data,pagination} (already the web SouStudent/PrPaginated shape); dropped the local
#     prisma.student query (+ the now-unused tenantId param, updated in pr-sou.controller.ts).
#   - pr-drive.service.ts `fetchStudents(ids)`: now hydrates each id from souGetAdminStudent via
#     Promise.allSettled (missing/failed id omitted → "Unknown" row) instead of prisma.student.findMany.
#     Used by the matrix/eligibility/grading/GD-roster views. (One SOU call per id — fine for drive-sized
#     cohorts.) Verified: /pr/sou/students returns the live SOU roster (33). Backend tsc 0. No FE change,
#     no migration. ⚠ Existing demo drives still show 0 students because they have NO registrations (and
#     sit under the orphaned pre-migration tenant) — assign students to a freshly-created drive to see it.

# Bugfix: PlaceReady New-drive audience = ONLY the students enrolled to the selected SOU campus drive
# (not the global roster, and nothing shown until a drive is picked). The builder's audience step listed
# the entire SOU roster regardless of / before the campus-drive selection. In SOU a campus drive is an
# Event; its enrolled students are the event's `assigned_students`. NEW backend endpoint
#   GET /api/pr/sou/events/:eventId/students  (staff-gated)
# → pr-sou.service `listEventStudents`: souGetEvent(eventId) → map assigned_students[].student →
#   SouStudent shape → { data, pagination } (best-effort; missing/empty → data:[]). Web builder
#   (DriveBuilder): audStudentsQuery repointed to souSharedService.listEventStudents(souEventId),
#   `enabled` only when a drive is selected; the search box now filters client-side over the loaded
#   enrolled set; a prompt ("Select a campus drive…") replaces any list pre-selection; onSelectEvent
#   clears the prior audience, and an effect pre-selects all enrolled students of the newly picked drive
#   (deselectable). Flows downstream unchanged: the drive's pr_drive_registrations (detail Students tab +
#   Overview "enrolled" count) are seeded from this audience, so the detail page shows only the enrolled
#   cohort too. Verified: /pr/sou/events/<demo>/students → 8, empty event → 0; backend tsc 0; web tsc 0 +
#   build clean. No migration.

# Enhancement: PlaceReady drive modules are sourced from the Test-Type master (add/remove/reorder/gate/
# configure), and drives are locked once Published. Previously step 2 of the drive builder used a hardcoded
# mock catalogue (DRIVE_MODULE_CATALOG) with only enable/disable. Now:
#  - Test-Type master gains an `evaluation` field (auto|ai|manual) = the module kind a drive uses it as.
#    PrTestType.evaluation String @default("auto"); test-type schema/service persist it + keep is_ai in
#    lockstep (is_ai = evaluation==='ai'); standard seeds set it (Group Discussion=manual, AI HR/AI
#    Communication=ai, rest=auto). Content → Test Types dialog's binary Standard/AI toggle replaced by a
#    3-way Evaluation selector (Auto-graded / AI-based / Manual) + a "Manual" list badge.
#  - Drive builder (DriveBuilder step 2): module list now comes from testTypeService.list({active:true}).
#    New drives start with NO modules; an "Add module" <select> lists active test types not already added
#    (dedupe by label — protects the loose module-key join to stage results); each module takes kind from
#    the type's evaluation, with a trash Remove button, up/down reorder, gate+threshold, and full config
#    (auto: questions/time/bank/attempts/difficulty-mix; the Bank <select> is filtered to the module's
#    test type; ai: time; manual: GD note). BuilderModule gained a UI-only `testTypeId`. The old per-module
#    enable/disable toggle + defaultModules() were removed (every listed module is active).
#  - Edit lock: updateDrive (pr-drive.service) throws BusinessRuleError PR_DRIVE_LOCKED (422) unless
#    status==='Draft'; createDrive/duplicateDrive/closeDrive unchanged. Detail page shows a disabled
#    "Editing locked" chip (Lock icon) instead of "Edit drive" when status!=='Draft'.
# Verified: backend tsc 0; web tsc 0 + build clean. (Live test pending the migration below.)
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED: schema.prisma adds PrTestType.evaluation (String @default("auto") @db.VarChar(10)).
#   Run in placeready/backend: `npx prisma migrate dev --name pr_test_type_evaluation`, restart :4100.
#   Then BACKFILL existing rows once (seed only inserts missing rows, won't fix existing):
#   UPDATE pr_test_types SET evaluation = CASE WHEN is_ai THEN 'ai'
#     WHEN lower(name) LIKE '%group discussion%' THEN 'manual' ELSE 'auto' END;

# Enhancement: Mobile practice-videos — server-side pagination + Category & Sub-category dropdown filters
# The Flutter student "Practice videos" screen fetched the full library and filtered client-side with
# category chips only (no sub-category, no pagination). Added opt-in server-side pagination to the

# Enhancement: PlaceReady AI-based test framework — real-time AI VOICE interviews (full pipeline)
# TPO admin configures an AI-based test in Content → Test Type Master: evaluation "AI-based" now has a
# working AI configuration — model dropdown (OpenAI gpt-4o-mini/gpt-4o + Anthropic Claude Haiku/Sonnet,
# server-driven registry GET /pr/ai/models), interview prompt (actually saved + prefilled on edit),
# source Question Bank picker, and reference PDF uploads (multipart, pdf-parse text extraction at
# upload, authenticated download; PrAiDoc child table). Drive builder AI modules now carry
# test_type_id + an Attempts control (1/2/3/5/Unlimited), both persisted on PrDriveModule.
# Students take the module as a LIVE VOICE INTERVIEW on mobile: backend POST /pr/interviews/start
# enforces EVERYTHING server-side (ai_assessments_enabled flag, drive Published + registration, gate
# locks via the shared buildStudentStages, attempt budget, wall clock: ends_at anchor + room-scoped
# LiveKit token TTL + 60s sweeper that expires overruns and scores partial transcripts), snapshots the
# full AI contract (guardrailed system prompt + bank questions + PDF excerpts + model) into
# PrInterviewSession.config_snapshot, creates a LiveKit Cloud room + explicit agent dispatch.
# NEW agent worker package placeready/agent (@livekit/agents, Node/TS): Deepgram Nova-3 STT + Aura-2
# TTS + the configured LLM; greets, interviews per the frozen prompt (hard rules: interview-only,
# injection-resistant, never reveals answers), wraps up at T−60s, hard-stops at T, streams the
# transcript to /api/pr/agent/* (shared-secret x-pr-agent-key auth, mounted above the JWT gate).
# On completion the backend scores the transcript (structured-output rubric: communication/technical/
# confidence/relevance/overall) → PrInterviewSession.score(+breakdown) → upserts PrStageResult
# (gate modules pass/fail by gate_threshold) → recomputeStudentReadiness, so funnel/matrix/
# eligibility/readiness light up unchanged. Web console: drive detail gains an "AI Interviews" tab
# (sessions, transcript bubbles, score breakdown, Rescore). Mobile: drive AI stage launches the
# rewired voice_interview_screen (livekit_client; live transcript via lk.transcription text streams,
# countdown, resume support, score polling → FeedbackReportScreen).
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED — migration 20261007060443_ai_interviews is ALREADY AUTHORED (includes a
# hand-written partial unique index "one live session per stage"); run:
#   cd placeready/backend && npx prisma migrate dev
# ⚠ Keys needed in placeready/backend/.env + placeready/agent/.env before the voice loop works:
#   LIVEKIT_URL/API_KEY/API_SECRET (LiveKit Cloud), OPENAI_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (optional),
#   DEEPGRAM_API_KEY (agent). PR_AGENT_SHARED_SECRET already generated + synced in both .envs.
# Agent worker: cd placeready/agent && npm run dev

# Enhancement: AI voice interview — NO resume; leaving the interview ends it and uses the attempt
# Previously a student who dropped out could rejoin the same attempt (agent waited 60s; /start
# returned the live session with a fresh token). Now any break ends the interview immediately:
# agent completes on ParticipantDisconnected (no grace), backend closes the LiveKit room on
# completion/expiry (old token can't rejoin), and /start never resumes — it closes any leftover live
# session first (joined → completed + scored + counts as an attempt; never joined → failed, not
# counted) and then applies the attempt limit. Agent "complete" is idempotent (no double scoring).
# Mobile: Resume button removed; preflight warns that leaving/switching apps/losing connection ends
# the attempt; switching away from the app (paused/detached, mobile only) ends the interview.
# active_session_id / resumed removed from API payloads + DTOs. No schema change, no migration.
placeready/agent/src/main.ts

placeready/backend/src/modules/pr-drives/pr-drive.service.ts
placeready/mobile/lib/core/pr_service.dart
placeready/mobile/lib/screens/voice_interview_screen.dart

# Bugfix: AI voice interview went silent after the student's first answer (Claude models)
# Live e2e run: the AI greeted + asked Q1, Deepgram transcribed the answer, then the agent died with
# anthropic.LLM APIConnectionError (retryable:false, recoverable:false) and the AgentSession closed —
# the student was left talking to silence. Cause: @livekit/agents-plugin-anthropic (bundled
# @anthropic-ai/sdk 0.33) converts a mid-stream interruption (student speaks over the AI) into a
# fatal non-retryable error. Fix: Claude now runs through @livekit/agents-plugin-openai against
# Anthropic's OpenAI-compatible endpoint (verified replying correctly). Hardening: agent listens for
# AgentSession Close — a pipeline death now ends the interview immediately (reason 'error') instead
# of silence; backend marks such sessions `failed` (transcript kept, NOT scored, attempt NOT used).
placeready/agent/src/main.ts
placeready/backend/src/modules/pr-interviews/pr-interview.service.ts

# Bugfix: AI interview stage showed "Completed" with no way to use remaining attempts
# The mobile drive screen only offered "Start" on not-started stages, so after one AI interview
# attempt the stage was terminal even when the module allowed more attempts. AI stages with
# attempts left (not gate-locked) now show a "Retake" button + "N of M attempts left — your latest
# attempt counts" (also on a failed AI gate stage). Backend still enforces the attempt budget.
placeready/mobile/lib/screens/drive_detail_screen.dart

# Bugfix: Claude via OpenAI-compatible endpoint rejected "tools: []" (AI stopped after first answer)
# Second live run: @livekit/agents-plugin-openai sends tools: [] when the agent has no function tools;
# OpenAI accepts it but Anthropic's OpenAI-compatible endpoint returns 400 "tools: List should have
# at least 1 item". New AnthropicCompatLLM (subclass of openai.LLM) strips the empty list via
# extra_body. Verified against Anthropic: plain → empty reply, fixed → correct reply. That attempt was
# correctly recorded as `failed` (not scored, attempt not used) by the earlier hardening.
placeready/agent/src/main.ts

# Bugfix: New drives' test stages showed "This stage isn't ready to take in the app yet"
# The drive builder stores a bank + question count + difficulty mix per auto-graded stage, but nothing
# ever built a test paper (PrAssessment) from it — only seeded demo drives had papers. Now, when a
# registered student opens a Published drive, each enabled bank-backed auto stage without a paper gets
# ONE shared paper built from its bank (num_questions, easy/medium/hard mix, time_min → duration,
# gate_threshold → cutoff). Only auto-gradable types with a usable answer key are drawn (single / multi /
# short families). Postgres advisory lock prevents duplicate papers on concurrent first opens.
# No schema change, no migration.

# Bugfix: Test stages ignored the drive's "attempts" setting — a failed test could never be retaken
# Retake existed only for AI-interview stages; a failed test stage was hard-locked ("no self-retry")
# and the backend never counted test attempts. Now startAttempt enforces the module's attempt budget for
# drive-linked papers (unset = 1, 99 = unlimited; every started attempt counts) → 422
# PR_ATTEMPTS_EXHAUSTED; the drive detail returns `attempts {attempts_allowed, attempts_used}` on test
# stages; the app shows "Retake · N of M attempts left — your latest attempt counts" (same rule as AI
# interviews) and a clear message once all attempts are used. No migration.

# Bugfix: Mock Interview — camera on Chrome, web upload, admin playback, and session-flow gaps
# Camera: on web the screen no longer pre-checks via permission_handler (its failure showed a dead-end
# "Open settings" screen that does nothing in a browser); initialising the camera shows ONE browser
# prompt for camera+mic, and camera errors map to clear states (blocked → "allow via the address-bar
# icon", no camera, camera busy, insecure page) — every state has "Try again". Phones keep the OS
# permission flow ("Open settings" only when permanently denied).
# Upload: sends the recording as bytes with an explicit video content type (fromFile cannot work on web,
# where the recording is a blob: URL). Backend accepts octet-stream when the filename has a media
# extension, rejects other types with 400 (was 500), cap 60→200 MB (oversize → 413), deletes orphan /
# replaced files on error or re-record.
# Admin playback: /api/pr/media now sends Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: cross-origin (helmet default
# blocked the console on :5174 from playing clips served by :4100).
# Flow: resume shows already-recorded answers; recording/submit only while in_progress on a Published
# interview; submit needs ≥1 answer; an answer's question must belong to the session's interview; staff
# can only review submitted sessions (console labels in-progress sessions and hides the rating);
# students only see drive-linked interviews for drives they're registered to, and keep seeing Closed

# Enhancement: PlaceReady client sheet — Batch D, question schema + paper engine (SR 38, 29, 30, 31, 32, 12)
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED — placeready/backend/prisma/schema.prisma:
#   new model PrSkill (pr_skills: tenant_id, name, is_active; unique tenant+name);
#   PrQuestion.skill_ids String[] @default([]) + PrQuestion.mandatory_order Int?;
#   PrDriveModule.bank_sources Json @default("[]");
#   PrTestType.ai_source_bank_ids String[] @default([]).
#   Run: cd placeready/backend && npx prisma migrate dev --name pr_skills_mandatory_multibank  (additive; legacy
#   bank_id / ai_source_bank_id kept as mirrors of the first bank, old rows fall back to them).
# SR 38 (tagging part): fixed skill master (Question Banks → Skills tab; /api/pr/skills, delete only when unused,
#   else deactivate); questions carry skill_ids (editor picker, bank filter + chips, import/download "Skills"
#   column by name, copy keeps tags); drive papers snapshot skills {id,name} in each question's config for the
#   skill-wise reports (Batch G).
# SR 29/30 (test side): question "Mandatory" toggle; bank editor "Mandatory questions" panel with up/down order
#   (PUT /api/pr/question-banks/:id/mandatory-order); import/download "Mandatory" column (order number / Yes).
#   Papers put each bank's mandatory questions first in that order, then fill the rest by the mix.
#   (AI-interview side → Batch E.)
# SR 31/32: a module draws from several banks, each with its own question count, in order (one paper section

# Enhancement: PlaceReady Batch F — Indian voice + multilingual AI interviews + live transcript (SR 24, 25, 26).
#   Voice pipeline moved from Deepgram (US-English STT default + American "Asteria" voice) to Sarvam AI via the
#   LiveKit Node plugin: Saaras v3 streaming STT in the chosen language with code-mix (Hinglish) handling, Bulbul v3
#   TTS with one fixed Indian voice (SARVAM_TTS_SPEAKER) that speaks every language. PR_VOICE_PROVIDER=deepgram
#   restores the old pipeline. Language: TPO ticks allowed languages per AI test type (default English, Hindi,
#   Gujarati; 10 Indian languages available); the student picks one on the interview start screen (remembered on
#   the phone); the system prompt gets a LANGUAGE section, scoring is told the language, the hard-stop goodbye is
#   spoken in hi/gu/en; Gujarati and other non-en/hi sessions use VAD turn detection. Sarvam-105B added to the AI
#   model list (OpenAI-compatible API, ₹15/₹60 per M tokens). Console shows the language per session; mobile
#   captions now render chunk-by-chunk while the interviewer speaks (SR 26) under a "Live transcript" header.
# ⚠ MIGRATION REQUIRED: placeready/backend/prisma/schema.prisma — PrTestType.ai_languages String[] (default
#   en-IN/hi-IN/gu-IN), PrInterviewSession.language. Run: cd placeready/backend && npx prisma migrate dev --name pr_interview_languages
# ⚠ NEW ENV: SARVAM_API_KEY in placeready/agent/.env (STT/TTS) AND placeready/backend/.env (Sarvam LLM scoring);
#   agent/.env optional PR_VOICE_PROVIDER, SARVAM_TTS_SPEAKER, SARVAM_TTS_MODEL. Agent: `npm install` (LiveKit agents
#   pinned to 1.9.4 + @livekit/agents-plugin-sarvam). Restart the agent worker and backend.
#   Key verified 2026-10-28: TTS + chat both live, no beta gating. Sarvam-105B is a THINKING model — both call sites
#   send reasoning_effort 'low' (the minimum Sarvam accepts), else the whole budget goes to hidden reasoning and
#   content comes back empty. bulbul:v4-flash rejects the v3 speaker names (needs e.g. amit_hi_conversational).

# Bugfix: PlaceReady AI interviewer stayed silent until the student spoke first. The kickoff reply after the
#   student joined sent the LLM a system-only request (no user turn): Claude rejects that (400) and Sarvam-105B
#   returns no content, so the greeting silently failed and the first "hello" was what started the interview.
#   The kickoff now runs on a one-off copy of the chat context carrying a synthetic user turn (en/hi/gu) that is
#   never committed — the transcript/captions still start with the interviewer. VAD model now prewarmed per worker
#   process (was loaded after the student joined). Restart the agent worker. No migration.
placeready/agent/src/main.ts
